If response is not a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error) and fetchParams’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-request)’s [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client) is a [secure context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#secure-context), then set timingInfo’s [server-timing headers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-timing-info-server-timing-headers) to the result of [getting, decoding, and splitting](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-get-decode-split) ``Server-Timing`` from response’s [internal response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-internal-response)’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list). 
Using _response_’s [internal response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-internal-response) is safe as exposing ``Server-Timing`` header data is guarded through the ``Timing-Allow-Origin`` header. 
The user agent may decide to expose ``Server-Timing`` headers to non-secure contexts requests as well. 
  3. If fetchParams’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-request)’s [destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-destination) is "`document`", then set fetchParams’s [controller](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-controller)’s [full timing info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-full-timing-info) to fetchParams’s [timing info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-timing-info). 
  4. Let processResponseEndOfBody be the following steps: 
    1. Let unsafeEndTime be the [unsafe shared current time](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dfn-unsafe-shared-current-time). 
    2. Set fetchParams’s [controller](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-controller)’s [report timing steps](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-report-timing-steps) to the following steps given a [global object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object) global: 
      1. If fetchParams’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-request)’s [URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url)’s [scheme](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-scheme) is not an [HTTP(S) scheme](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#http-scheme), then return. 
      2. Set timingInfo’s [end time](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-timing-info-end-time) to the [relative high resolution time](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dfn-relative-high-resolution-time) given unsafeEndTime and global. 
      3. Let cacheState be response’s [cache state](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-cache-state). 
      4. Let bodyInfo be response’s [body info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body-info). 
      5. If response’s [timing allow passed flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-timing-allow-passed) is not set, then set timingInfo to the result of [creating an opaque timing info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#create-an-opaque-timing-info) for timingInfo and set cacheState to the empty string. 
This covers the case of response being a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
      6. Let responseStatus be 0. 
      7. If fetchParams’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-request)’s [mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-mode) is not "`navigate`" or response’s [redirect taint](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response-redirect-taint) is "`same-origin`": 
        1. Set responseStatus to response’s [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status). 
        2. Let mimeType be the result of [extracting a MIME type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-extract-mime-type) from response’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list). 
        3. If mimeType is not failure, then set bodyInfo’s [content type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response-body-info-content-type) to the result of [minimizing a supported MIME type](https://mimesniff.spec.whatwg.org/#minimize-a-supported-mime-type) given mimeType. 
      8. If fetchParams’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-request)’s [initiator type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-initiator-type) is non-null, then [mark resource timing](https://w3c.github.io/resource-timing/#dfn-mark-resource-timing) given timingInfo, fetchParams’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-request)’s [URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url), fetchParams’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-request)’s [initiator type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-initiator-type), global, cacheState, bodyInfo, and responseStatus. 
    3. Let processResponseEndOfBodyTask be the following steps: 
      1. Set fetchParams’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-request)’s [done flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#done-flag). 
      2. If fetchParams’s [process response end-of-body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-process-response-end-of-body) is non-null, then run fetchParams’s [process response end-of-body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-process-response-end-of-body) given response. 
      3. If fetchParams’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-request)’s [initiator type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-initiator-type) is non-null and fetchParams’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-request)’s [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client)’s [global object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global) is fetchParams’s [task destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-task-destination), then run fetchParams’s [controller](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-controller)’s [report timing steps](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-report-timing-steps) given fetchParams’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-request)’s [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client)’s [global object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global). 
    4. [Queue a fetch task](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#queue-a-fetch-task) to run processResponseEndOfBodyTask with fetchParams’s [task destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-task-destination). 
  5. If fetchParams’s [process response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-process-response) is non-null, then [queue a fetch task](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#queue-a-fetch-task) to run fetchParams’s [process response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-process-response) given response, with fetchParams’s [task destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-task-destination). 
  6. Let internalResponse be response, if response is a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error); otherwise response’s [internal response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-internal-response). 
  7. If response is a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error), then run the [WebDriver BiDi fetch error](https://w3c.github.io/webdriver-bidi/#webdriver-bidi-fetch-error) steps with request. Otherwise, run the [WebDriver BiDi response completed](https://w3c.github.io/webdriver-bidi/#webdriver-bidi-response-completed) steps with request and response. 
  8. If internalResponse’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body) is null, then run processResponseEndOfBody. 
  9. Otherwise:
    1. Let transformStream be a new `TransformStream[](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#transformstream)`. 
    2. Let identityTransformAlgorithm be an algorithm which, given chunk, [enqueues](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#transformstream-enqueue) chunk in transformStream. 
    3. [Set up](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#transformstream-set-up) transformStream with [_transformAlgorithm_](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#transformstream-set-up-transformalgorithm) set to identityTransformAlgorithm and [_flushAlgorithm_](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#transformstream-set-up-flushalgorithm) set to processResponseEndOfBody. 
    4. Set internalResponse’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body)’s [stream](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-stream) to the result of internalResponse’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body)’s [stream](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-stream) [piped through](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-pipe-through) transformStream. 
This `TransformStream[](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#transformstream)` is needed for the purpose of receiving a notification when the stream reaches its end, and is otherwise an [identity transform stream](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#identity-transform-stream). 
  10. If fetchParams’s [process response consume body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-process-response-consume-body) is non-null, then: 
    1. Let processBody given nullOrBytes be this step: run fetchParams’s [process response consume body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-process-response-consume-body) given response and nullOrBytes. 
    2. Let processBodyError be this step: run fetchParams’s [process response consume body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-process-response-consume-body) given response and failure. 
    3. If internalResponse’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body) is null, then [queue a fetch task](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#queue-a-fetch-task) to run processBody given null, with fetchParams’s [task destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-task-destination). 
    4. Otherwise, [fully read](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#body-fully-read) internalResponse’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body) given processBody, processBodyError, and fetchParams’s [task destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-task-destination). 


###  4.2. Override fetch
To override fetch, given "`scheme-fetch`" or "`http-fetch`" type, a [fetch params](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params) fetchParams, and an optional boolean makeCORSPreflight (default false): 
  1. Let request be fetchParams’ [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-request). 
  2. Let response be the result of executing [potentially override response for a request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#potentially-override-response-for-a-request) on request. 
  3. If response is non-null, then return response. 
  4. Switch on type and run the associated step:  

"`scheme fetch`" 
    
Set response be the result of running [scheme fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-scheme-fetch) given fetchParams.  

"`HTTP fetch`" 
    
Set response be the result of running [HTTP fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-http-fetch) given fetchParams and makeCORSPreflight. 
  5. Return response. 


The potentially override response for a request algorithm takes a [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request) request, and returns either a [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response) or null. Its behavior is [implementation-defined](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#implementation-defined), allowing user agents to intervene on the [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request) by returning a response directly, or allowing the request to proceed by returning null. 
By default, the algorithm has the following trivial implementation: 
  1. Return null. 


User agents will generally override this default implementation with a somewhat more complex set of behaviors. For example, a user agent might decide that its users' safety is best preserved by generally blocking requests to `https://unsafe.example/`, while synthesizing a shim for the widely-used resource `https://unsafe.example/widget.js` to avoid breakage. That implementation might look like the following: 
  1. If request’s [current url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-current-url)’s [host](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-host)’s [registrable domain](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#host-registrable-domain) is "`unsafe.example`": 
    1. If request’s [current url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-current-url)’s [path](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-path) is « "`widget.js`" »: 
      1. Let body be [_insert a byte sequence representing the shimmed content here_]. 
      2. Return a new [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response) with the following properties:  

[type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-type) 
    "`cors`"  

[status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status) 
    200  

... 
    ...  

[body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body) 
    The result of getting body [as a body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-sequence-as-a-body). 
    2. Return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
  2. Return null. 


###  4.3. Scheme fetch
To scheme fetch, given a [fetch params](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params) fetchParams: 
  1. If fetchParams is [canceled](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-canceled), then return the [appropriate network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#appropriate-network-error) for fetchParams. 
  2. Let request be fetchParams’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-request). 
  3. Switch on request’s [current URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-current-url)’s [scheme](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-scheme) and run the associated steps:  

"`about`" 
    
If request’s [current URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-current-url)’s [path](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-path) is the string "`blank`", then return a new [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response) whose [status message](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status-message) is ``OK``, [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list) is « (``Content-Type``, ``text/html;charset=utf-8``) », and [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body) is the empty byte sequence [as a body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-sequence-as-a-body). 
[URLs](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url) such as "`about:config`" are handled during [navigation](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsing-the-web.html#navigate) and result in a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error) in the context of [fetching](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch).  

"`blob`" 
    
    1. Let blobURLEntry be request’s [current URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-current-url)’s [blob URL entry](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-blob-entry). 
    2. If request’s [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method) is not ``GET`` or blobURLEntry is null, then return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). [[FILEAPI]](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#biblio-fileapi "File API")
The ``GET`` [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-method) restriction serves no useful purpose other than being interoperable. 
    3. Let requestEnvironment be the result of [determining the environment](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-determine-the-environment) given request. 
    4. Let isTopLevelSelfFetch be false. 
    5. If request’s [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client) is non-null: 
      1. Let global be request’s [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client)’s [global object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global). 
      2. If all of the following conditions are true: 
         * global is a `Window[](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window)` object; 
         * global’s [navigable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window-navigable) is not null; 
         * global’s [navigable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window-navigable)’s [parent](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-parent) is null; and 
         * requestEnvironment’s [creation URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-environment-creation-url) [equals](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-equals) request’s [current URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-current-url), 
then set isTopLevelSelfFetch to true. 
    6. Let stringOrEnvironment be the result of these steps: 
      1. If request’s [destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-destination) is "`document`", then return "`top-level-navigation`". 
      2. If isTopLevelSelfFetch is true, then return "`top-level-self-fetch`". 
      3. Return requestEnvironment. 
    7. Let blob be the result of [obtaining a blob object](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#blob-url-obtain-object) given blobURLEntry and stringOrEnvironment. 
    8. If blob is not a `Blob[](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#dfn-Blob)` object, then return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
    9. Let response be a new [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response). 
    10. Let fullLength be blob’s `size[](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#dfn-size)`. 
    11. Let serializedFullLength be fullLength, [serialized](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#serialize-an-integer) and [isomorphic encoded](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#isomorphic-encode). 
    12. Let type be blob’s `type[](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#dfn-type)`. 
    13. If request’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list) [does not contain](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#header-list-contains) ``Range``: 
      1. Let bodyWithType be the result of [safely extracting](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#bodyinit-safely-extract) blob. 
      2. Set response’s [status message](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status-message) to ``OK``. 
      3. Set response’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body) to bodyWithType’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#body-with-type-body). 
      4. Set response’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list) to « (``Content-Length``, serializedFullLength), (``Content-Type``, type) ». 
    14. Otherwise: 
      1. Set response’s [range-requested flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-range-requested-flag). 
      2. Let rangeHeader be the result of [getting](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-get) ``Range`` from request’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list). 
      3. Let rangeValue be the result of [parsing a single range header value](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#simple-range-header-value) given rangeHeader and true. 
      4. If rangeValue is failure, then return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
      5. Let (rangeStart, rangeEnd) be rangeValue. 
      6. If rangeStart is null: 
        1. Set rangeStart to fullLength − rangeEnd. 
        2. Set rangeEnd to rangeStart + rangeEnd − 1. 
      7. Otherwise: 
        1. If rangeStart is greater than or equal to fullLength, then return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
        2. If rangeEnd is null or rangeEnd is greater than or equal to fullLength, then set rangeEnd to fullLength − 1. 
      8. Let slicedBlob be the result of invoking [slice blob](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#slice-blob) given blob, rangeStart, rangeEnd + 1, and type. 
A range header denotes an inclusive byte range, while the [slice blob](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#slice-blob) algorithm input range does not. To use the [slice blob](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#slice-blob) algorithm, we have to increment rangeEnd. 
      9. Let slicedBodyWithType be the result of [safely extracting](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#bodyinit-safely-extract) slicedBlob. 
      10. Set response’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body) to slicedBodyWithType’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#body-with-type-body). 
      11. Let serializedSlicedLength be slicedBlob’s `size[](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#dfn-size)`, [serialized](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#serialize-an-integer) and [isomorphic encoded](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#isomorphic-encode). 
      12. Let contentRange be the result of invoking [build a content range](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#build-a-content-range) given rangeStart, rangeEnd, and fullLength. 
      13. Set response’s [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status) to 206. 
      14. Set response’s [status message](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status-message) to ``Partial Content``. 
      15. Set response’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list) to « (``Content-Length``, serializedSlicedLength), (``Content-Type``, type), (``Content-Range``, contentRange) ». 
    15. Return response.  

"`data`" 
    
    1. Let dataURLStruct be the result of running the [`data:` URL processor](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#data-url-processor) on request’s [current URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-current-url). 
    2. If dataURLStruct is failure, then return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
    3. Let mimeType be dataURLStruct’s [MIME type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#data-url-struct-mime-type), [serialized](https://mimesniff.spec.whatwg.org/#serialize-a-mime-type-to-bytes). 
    4. Return a new [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response) whose [status message](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status-message) is ``OK``, [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list) is « (``Content-Type``, mimeType) », and [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body) is dataURLStruct’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#data-url-struct-body) [as a body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-sequence-as-a-body).  

"`file`" 
    
For now, unfortunate as it is, `file:` [URLs](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url) are left as an exercise for the reader. 
When in doubt, return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error).  

[HTTP(S) scheme](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#http-scheme) 
    
Return the result of running [HTTP fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-http-fetch) given fetchParams. 
  4. Return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 


To determine the environment, given a [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request) request: 
  1. If request’s [reserved client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-reserved-client) is non-null, then return request’s [reserved client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-reserved-client). 
  2. If request’s [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client) is non-null, then return request’s [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client). 
  3. Return null. 


###  4.4. HTTP fetch
To HTTP fetch, given a [fetch params](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params) fetchParams and an optional boolean makeCORSPreflight (default false), run these steps: 
  1. Let request be fetchParams’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-request). 
  2. Let response and internalResponse be null. 
  3. If request’s [service-workers mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-service-workers-mode) is "`all`", then: 
    1. Let requestForServiceWorker be a [clone](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-clone) of request. 
    2. If requestForServiceWorker’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body) is non-null, then: 
      1. Let transformStream be a new `TransformStream[](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#transformstream)`. 
      2. Let transformAlgorithm given chunk be these steps: 
        1. If fetchParams is [canceled](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-canceled), then abort these steps. 
        2. If chunk is not a `Uint8Array[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-Uint8Array)` object, then [terminate](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-terminate) fetchParams’s [controller](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-controller). 
        3. Otherwise, [enqueue](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-enqueue) chunk in transformStream. The user agent may split the chunk into [implementation-defined](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#implementation-defined) practical sizes and [enqueue](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-enqueue) each of them. The user agent also may concatenate the chunks into an [implementation-defined](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#implementation-defined) practical size and [enqueue](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-enqueue) it. 
      3. [Set up](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#transformstream-set-up) transformStream with [_transformAlgorithm_](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#transformstream-set-up-transformalgorithm) set to transformAlgorithm. 
      4. Set requestForServiceWorker’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body)’s [stream](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-stream) to the result of requestForServiceWorker’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body)’s [stream](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-stream) [piped through](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-pipe-through) transformStream. 
    3. Let serviceWorkerStartTime be the [coarsened shared current time](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dfn-coarsened-shared-current-time) given fetchParams’s [cross-origin isolated capability](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-cross-origin-isolated-capability). 
    4. Let fetchResponse be the result of invoking [handle fetch](https://w3c.github.io/ServiceWorker/#handle-fetch) for requestForServiceWorker, with fetchParams’s [controller](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-controller) and fetchParams’s [cross-origin isolated capability](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-cross-origin-isolated-capability). [[HTML]](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#biblio-html "HTML Standard") [[SW]](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#biblio-sw "Service Workers Nightly")
    5. If fetchResponse is a [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response): 
      1. Set response to fetchResponse. 
      2. Set fetchParams’s [timing info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-timing-info)’s [final service worker start time](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-timing-info-final-service-worker-start-time) to serviceWorkerStartTime. 
      3. Set fetchParams’s [timing info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-timing-info)’s [service worker timing info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-timing-info-service-worker-timing-info) to response’s [service worker timing info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response-service-worker-timing-info). 
      4. If request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) is non-null, then [cancel](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-cancel) request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) with undefined. 
      5. Set internalResponse to response, if response is not a [filtered response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-filtered-response); otherwise to response’s [internal response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-internal-response). 
      6. Run the [WebDriver BiDi response started](https://w3c.github.io/webdriver-bidi/#webdriver-bidi-response-started) steps with request and response. 
      7. If one of the following is true 
         * response’s [type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-type) is "`error`" 
         * request’s [mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-mode) is "`same-origin`" and response’s [type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-type) is "`cors`" 
         * request’s [mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-mode) is not "`no-cors`" and response’s [type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-type) is "`opaque`" 
         * request’s [redirect mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-redirect-mode) is not "`manual`" and response’s [type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-type) is "`opaqueredirect`" 
         * request’s [redirect mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-redirect-mode) is not "`follow`" and response’s [URL list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-url-list) has more than one item 
then return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
    6. Otherwise, if fetchResponse is a [service worker timing info](https://w3c.github.io/ServiceWorker/#service-worker-timing-info), then set fetchParams’s [timing info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-timing-info)’s [service worker timing info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-timing-info-service-worker-timing-info) to fetchResponse. 
  4. If response is null, then: 
    1. If makeCORSPreflight is true and one of these conditions is true: 
       * There is no [method cache entry match](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-match-method) for request’s [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method) using request, and either request’s [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method) is not a [CORS-safelisted method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cors-safelisted-method) or request’s [use-CORS-preflight flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#use-cors-preflight-flag) is set. 
       * There is at least one [item](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item) in the [CORS-unsafe request-header names](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cors-unsafe-request-header-names) with request’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list) for which there is no [header-name cache entry match](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-match-header) using request. 
Then: 
      1. Let preflightResponse be the result of running [CORS-preflight fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cors-preflight-fetch-0) given request. 
      2. If preflightResponse is a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error), then return preflightResponse. 
This step checks the [CORS-preflight cache](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache) and if there is no suitable entry it performs a [CORS-preflight fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cors-preflight-fetch-0) which, if successful, populates the cache. The purpose of the [CORS-preflight fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cors-preflight-fetch-0) is to ensure the [fetched](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch) resource is familiar with the [CORS protocol](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cors-protocol). The cache is there to minimize the number of [CORS-preflight fetches](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cors-preflight-fetch-0). 
    2. If request’s [redirect mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-redirect-mode) is "`follow`", then set request’s [service-workers mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-service-workers-mode) to "`none`". 
Redirects coming from the network (as opposed to from a service worker) are not to be exposed to a service worker. 
    3. Set response and internalResponse to the result of running [HTTP-network-or-cache fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-http-network-or-cache-fetch) given fetchParams. 
    4. If request’s [response tainting](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-response-tainting) is "`cors`" and a [CORS check](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cors-check) for request and response returns failure, then return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
As the [CORS check](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cors-check) is not to be applied to [responses](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response) whose [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status) is 304 or 407, or [responses](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response) from a service worker for that matter, it is applied here. 
    5. If the [TAO check](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-tao-check) for request and response returns failure, then set request’s [timing allow failed flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#timing-allow-failed). 
  5. If either request’s [response tainting](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-response-tainting) or response’s [type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-type) is "`opaque`", and the [cross-origin resource policy check](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cross-origin-resource-policy-check) with request’s [origin](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-origin), request’s [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client), request’s [destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-destination), and internalResponse returns **blocked** , then return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
The [cross-origin resource policy check](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cross-origin-resource-policy-check) runs for responses coming from the network and responses coming from the service worker. This is different from the [CORS check](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cors-check), as request’s [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client) and the service worker can have different embedder policies. 
  6. If internalResponse’s [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status) is a [redirect status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#redirect-status): 
    1. If request is a [navigation request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#navigation-request), then [append to a request’s navigation timing allow values list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#append-to-a-requests-navigation-timing-allow-values-list) given request and internalResponse. 
    2. If internalResponse’s [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status) is not 303, request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) is non-null, and the [connection](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-connection) uses HTTP/2, then user agents may, and are even encouraged to, transmit an `RST_STREAM` frame. 
303 is excluded as certain communities ascribe special status to it. 
    3. Switch on request’s [redirect mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-redirect-mode):  

"`error`" 
    
      1. Set response to a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 

"`manual`" 
    
      1. If request’s [mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-mode) is "`navigate`", then set fetchParams’s [controller](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-controller)’s [next manual redirect steps](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-next-manual-redirect-steps) to run [HTTP-redirect fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-http-redirect-fetch) given fetchParams and response. 
      2. Otherwise, set response to an [opaque-redirect filtered response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-filtered-response-opaque-redirect) whose [internal response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-internal-response) is internalResponse.  

"`follow`" 
    
      1. Run the [WebDriver BiDi response completed](https://w3c.github.io/webdriver-bidi/#webdriver-bidi-response-completed) steps with request and response. 
      2. Set response to the result of running [HTTP-redirect fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-http-redirect-fetch) given fetchParams and response. 
  7. Return response. Typically internalResponse’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body)’s [stream](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-stream) is still being enqueued to after returning.


###  4.5. HTTP-redirect fetch
To HTTP-redirect fetch, given a [fetch params](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params) fetchParams and a [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response) response, run these steps: 
  1. Let request be fetchParams’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-request). 
  2. Let internalResponse be response, if response is not a [filtered response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-filtered-response); otherwise response’s [internal response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-internal-response). 
  3. Let locationURL be internalResponse’s [location URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-location-url) given request’s [current URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-current-url)’s [fragment](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-fragment). 
  4. If locationURL is null, then return response. 
  5. If locationURL is failure, then return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
  6. If locationURL’s [scheme](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-scheme) is not an [HTTP(S) scheme](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#http-scheme), then return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
  7. If request’s [redirect count](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-redirect-count) is 20, then return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
  8. Increase request’s [redirect count](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-redirect-count) by 1. 
  9. If request’s [mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-mode) is "`cors`", locationURL [includes credentials](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#include-credentials), and request’s [origin](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-origin) is not [same origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#same-origin) with locationURL’s [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin), then return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
  10. If request’s [response tainting](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-response-tainting) is "`cors`" and locationURL [includes credentials](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#include-credentials), then return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
This catches a cross-origin resource redirecting to a same-origin URL. 
  11. If internalResponse’s [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status) is not 303, request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) is non-null, and request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body)’s [source](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-source) is null, then return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
  12. If one of the following is true 
     * internalResponse’s [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status) is 301 or 302 and request’s [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method) is ``POST`` 
     * internalResponse’s [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status) is 303 and request’s [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method) is not ``GET`` or ``HEAD`` 
then: 
    1. Set request’s [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method) to ``GET`` and request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) to null. 
    2. [For each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) headerName of [request-body-header name](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-body-header-name), [delete](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-delete) headerName from request’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list). 
  13. If request’s [current URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-current-url)’s [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin) is not [same origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#same-origin) with locationURL’s [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin), then [for each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) headerName of [CORS non-wildcard request-header name](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cors-non-wildcard-request-header-name), [delete](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-delete) headerName from request’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list). 
I.e., the moment another origin is seen after the initial request, the ``Authorization`` header is removed. 
  14. If request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) is non-null, then set request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) to the [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#body-with-type-body) of the result of [safely extracting](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#bodyinit-safely-extract) request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body)’s [source](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-source). 
request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body)’s [source](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-source)’s nullity has already been checked. 
  15. Let timingInfo be fetchParams’s [timing info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-timing-info). 
  16. Set timingInfo’s [redirect end time](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-timing-info-redirect-end-time) and [post-redirect start time](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-timing-info-post-redirect-start-time) to the [coarsened shared current time](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dfn-coarsened-shared-current-time) given fetchParams’s [cross-origin isolated capability](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-cross-origin-isolated-capability). 
  17. If timingInfo’s [redirect start time](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-timing-info-redirect-start-time) is 0, then set timingInfo’s [redirect start time](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-timing-info-redirect-start-time) to timingInfo’s [start time](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-timing-info-start-time). 
  18. [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append) locationURL to request’s [URL list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url-list). 
  19. Invoke [set request’s referrer policy on redirect](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-referrer-policy/#set-requests-referrer-policy-on-redirect) on request and internalResponse. [[REFERRER]](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#biblio-referrer "Referrer Policy")
  20. Let recursive be true. 
  21. If request’s [redirect mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-redirect-mode) is "`manual`", then: 
    1. [Assert](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert): request’s [mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-mode) is "`navigate`". 
    2. Set recursive to false. 
  22. Return the result of running [main fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-main-fetch) given fetchParams and recursive. 
This has to invoke [main fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-main-fetch) to get request’s [response tainting](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-response-tainting) correct. 


###  4.6. HTTP-network-or-cache fetch
To HTTP-network-or-cache fetch, given a [fetch params](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params) fetchParams, an optional boolean isAuthenticationFetch (default false), and an optional boolean isNewConnectionFetch (default false), run these steps: 
Some implementations might support caching of partial content, as per HTTP Caching. However, this is not widely supported by browser caches. [[HTTP-CACHING]](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#biblio-http-caching "HTTP Caching")
  1. Let request be fetchParams’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-request). 
  2. Let httpFetchParams be null. 
  3. Let httpRequest be null. 
  4. Let response be null. 
  5. Let storedResponse be null. 
  6. Let httpCache be null. 
  7. Let the revalidatingFlag be unset. 
  8. Run these steps, but [abort when](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#abort-when) fetchParams is [canceled](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-canceled): 
    1. If request’s [traversable for user prompts](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-window) is "`no-traversable`" and request’s [redirect mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-redirect-mode) is "`error`", then set httpFetchParams to fetchParams and httpRequest to request. 
    2. Otherwise: 
      1. Set httpRequest to a [clone](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-clone) of request. 
Implementations are encouraged to avoid teeing request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body)’s [stream](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-stream) when request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body)’s [source](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-source) is null as only a single body is needed in that case. E.g., when request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body)’s [source](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-source) is null, redirects and authentication will end up failing the fetch. 
      2. Set httpFetchParams to a copy of fetchParams. 
      3. Set httpFetchParams’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-request) to httpRequest. 
If user prompts or redirects are possible, then the user agent might need to re-send the request with a new set of headers after the user answers the prompt or the redirect location is determined. At that time, the original request body might have been partially sent already, so we need to clone the request (including the body) beforehand so that we have a spare copy available. 
    3. Let includeCredentials be true if one of 
       * request’s [credentials mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-credentials-mode) is "`include`" 
       * request’s [credentials mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-credentials-mode) is "`same-origin`" and request’s [response tainting](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-response-tainting) is "`basic`" 
is true; otherwise false. 
    4. If [Cross-Origin-Embedder-Policy allows credentials](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cross-origin-embedder-policy-allows-credentials) with request returns false, then set includeCredentials to false. 
    5. Let contentLength be httpRequest’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body)’s [length](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-total-bytes), if httpRequest’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) is non-null; otherwise null. 
    6. Let contentLengthHeaderValue be null. 
    7. If httpRequest’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) is null and httpRequest’s [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method) is ``POST`` or ``PUT``, then set contentLengthHeaderValue to ``0``. 
    8. If contentLength is non-null, then set contentLengthHeaderValue to contentLength, [serialized](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#serialize-an-integer) and [isomorphic encoded](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#isomorphic-encode). 
    9. If contentLengthHeaderValue is non-null, then [append](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-append) (``Content-Length``, contentLengthHeaderValue) to httpRequest’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list). 
    10. If contentLength is non-null and httpRequest’s [keepalive](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-keepalive-flag) is true, then: 
      1. Let inflightKeepaliveBytes be 0. 
      2. Let group be httpRequest’s [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client)’s [fetch group](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#environment-settings-object-fetch-group). 
      3. Let inflightRecords be the set of [fetch records](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch-record) in group whose [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch-record-request)’s [keepalive](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-keepalive-flag) is true and [done flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#done-flag) is unset. 
      4. [For each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) fetchRecord of inflightRecords: 
        1. Let inflightRequest be fetchRecord’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch-record-request). 
        2. Increment inflightKeepaliveBytes by inflightRequest’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body)’s [length](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-total-bytes). 
      5. If the sum of contentLength and inflightKeepaliveBytes is greater than 64 kibibytes, then return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
The above limit ensures that requests that are allowed to outlive the [environment settings object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object) and contain a body, have a bounded size and are not allowed to stay alive indefinitely. 
    11. If httpRequest’s [referrer](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-referrer) is a [URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url), then: 
      1. Let referrerValue be httpRequest’s [referrer](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-referrer), [serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer) and [isomorphic encoded](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#isomorphic-encode). 
      2. [Append](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-append) (``Referer``, referrerValue) to httpRequest’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list). 
    12. [Append a request ``Origin`` header](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#append-a-request-origin-header) for httpRequest. 
    13. [Append the Fetch metadata headers for httpRequest](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-fetch-metadata/#abstract-opdef-append-the-fetch-metadata-headers-for-a-request). [[FETCH-METADATA]](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#biblio-fetch-metadata "Fetch Metadata Request Headers")
    14. If httpRequest’s [initiator](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-initiator) is "`prefetch`", then [set a structured field value](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-set-structured-header) given (`[`Sec-Purpose`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#http-sec-purpose)`, the [token](https://httpwg.org/specs/rfc9651.html#token) `prefetch`) in httpRequest’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list). 
    15. If httpRequest’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list) [does not contain](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#header-list-contains) ``User-Agent``, then user agents should: 
      1. Let userAgent be httpRequest’s [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client)’s [environment default ``User-Agent`` value](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#environment-default-user-agent-value). 
      2. [Append](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-append) (``User-Agent``, userAgent) to httpRequest’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list). 
    16. If httpRequest’s [cache mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-cache-mode) is "`default`" and httpRequest’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list) [contains](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#header-list-contains) ``If-Modified-Since``, ``If-None-Match``, ``If-Unmodified-Since``, ``If-Match``, or ``If-Range``, then set httpRequest’s [cache mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-cache-mode) to "`no-store`". 
    17. If httpRequest’s [cache mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-cache-mode) is "`no-cache`", httpRequest’s [prevent no-cache cache-control header modification flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#no-cache-prevent-cache-control) is unset, and httpRequest’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list) [does not contain](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#header-list-contains) ``Cache-Control``, then [append](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-append) (``Cache-Control``, ``max-age=0``) to httpRequest’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list). 
    18. If httpRequest’s [cache mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-cache-mode) is "`no-store`" or "`reload`", then: 
      1. If httpRequest’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list) [does not contain](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#header-list-contains) ``Pragma``, then [append](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-append) (``Pragma``, ``no-cache``) to httpRequest’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list). 
      2. If httpRequest’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list) [does not contain](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#header-list-contains) ``Cache-Control``, then [append](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-append) (``Cache-Control``, ``no-cache``) to httpRequest’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list). 
    19. If httpRequest’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list) [contains](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#header-list-contains) ``Range``, then [append](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-append) (``Accept-Encoding``, ``identity``) to httpRequest’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list). 
This avoids a failure when [handling content codings](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#handle-content-codings) with a part of an encoded [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response). 
Additionally, [many servers](https://jakearchibald.github.io/accept-encoding-range-test/) mistakenly ignore ``Range`` headers if a non-identity encoding is accepted. 
    20. Modify httpRequest’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list) per HTTP. Do not [append](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-append) a given [header](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header) if httpRequest’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list) [contains](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#header-list-contains) that [header](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header)’s [name](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-name). 
It would be great if we could make this more normative somehow. At this point [headers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header) such as ``Accept-Encoding``, ``Connection``, ``DNT``, and ``Host``, are to be [appended](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-append) if necessary. 
``Accept``, ``Accept-Charset``, and ``Accept-Language`` must not be included at this point. 
``Accept`` and ``Accept-Language`` are already included (unless [`fetch()`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-global-fetch) is used, which does not include the latter by default), and ``Accept-Charset`` is a waste of bytes. See [HTTP header layer division](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#http-header-layer-division) for more details. 
    21. If includeCredentials is true, then: 
      1. [Append a request ``Cookie`` header](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#append-a-request-cookie-header) for httpRequest. 
      2. If httpRequest’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list) [does not contain](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#header-list-contains) ``Authorization``, then: 
        1. Let authorizationValue be null. 
        2. If there’s an [authentication entry](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#authentication-entry) for httpRequest and either httpRequest’s [use-URL-credentials flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-use-url-credentials-flag) is unset or httpRequest’s [current URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-current-url) does not [include credentials](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#include-credentials), then set authorizationValue to [authentication entry](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#authentication-entry). 
        3. Otherwise, if httpRequest’s [current URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-current-url) does [include credentials](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#include-credentials) and isAuthenticationFetch is true, set authorizationValue to httpRequest’s [current URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-current-url), converted to an ``Authorization`` value. 
        4. If authorizationValue is non-null, then [append](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-append) (``Authorization``, authorizationValue) to httpRequest’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list). 
    22. If there’s a [proxy-authentication entry](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#proxy-authentication-entry), use it as appropriate. 
This intentionally does not depend on httpRequest’s [credentials mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-credentials-mode). 
    23. Run the [WebDriver BiDi before request sent](https://w3c.github.io/webdriver-bidi/#webdriver-bidi-before-request-sent) steps with request. 
    24. Set httpCache to the result of [determining the HTTP cache partition](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#determine-the-http-cache-partition), given httpRequest. 
    25. If httpCache is null, then set httpRequest’s [cache mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-cache-mode) to "`no-store`". 
    26. If httpRequest’s [cache mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-cache-mode) is neither "`no-store`" nor "`reload`", then: 
      1. Set storedResponse to the result of selecting a response from the httpCache, possibly needing validation, as per the "[Constructing Responses from Caches](https://httpwg.org/specs/rfc9111.html#constructing.responses.from.caches)" chapter of HTTP Caching, if any. [[HTTP-CACHING]](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#biblio-http-caching "HTTP Caching")
As mandated by HTTP, this still takes the ``Vary`` [header](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header) into account. 
      2. If storedResponse is non-null, then: 
        1. If [cache mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-cache-mode) is "`default`", storedResponse is a [stale-while-revalidate response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-stale-while-revalidate-response), and httpRequest’s [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client) is non-null, then: 
          1. Set response to storedResponse. 
          2. Set response’s [cache state](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-cache-state) to "`local`". 
          3. Let revalidateRequest be a [clone](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-clone) of request. 
          4. Set revalidateRequest’s [cache mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-cache-mode) set to "`no-cache`". 
          5. Set revalidateRequest’s [prevent no-cache cache-control header modification flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#no-cache-prevent-cache-control). 
          6. Set revalidateRequest’s [service-workers mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-service-workers-mode) set to "`none`". 
          7. [In parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel), run [main fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-main-fetch) given a new [fetch params](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params) whose [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-request) is revalidateRequest. 
This fetch is only meant to update the state of httpCache and the response will be unused until another cache access. The stale response will be used as the response to current request. This fetch is issued in the context of a client so if it goes away the request will be terminated. 
        2. Otherwise: 
          1. If storedResponse is a [stale response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-stale-response), then set the revalidatingFlag. 
          2. If the revalidatingFlag is set and httpRequest’s [cache mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-cache-mode) is neither "`force-cache`" nor "`only-if-cached`", then: 
            1. If storedResponse’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list) [contains](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#header-list-contains) ``ETag``, then [append](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-append) (``If-None-Match``, ``ETag``'s [value](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-value)) to httpRequest’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list). 
            2. If storedResponse’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list) [contains](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#header-list-contains) ``Last-Modified``, then [append](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-append) (``If-Modified-Since``, ``Last-Modified``'s [value](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-value)) to httpRequest’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list). 
See also the "[Sending a Validation Request](https://httpwg.org/specs/rfc9111.html#validation.sent)" chapter of HTTP Caching. [[HTTP-CACHING]](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#biblio-http-caching "HTTP Caching")
          3. Otherwise, set response to storedResponse and set response’s [cache state](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-cache-state) to "`local`". 
  9. [If aborted](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#if-aborted), then return the [appropriate network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#appropriate-network-error) for fetchParams. 
  10. If response is not null, then run the [WebDriver BiDi response started](https://w3c.github.io/webdriver-bidi/#webdriver-bidi-response-started) steps with request and response. 
  11. If response is null, then: 
    1. If httpRequest’s [cache mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-cache-mode) is "`only-if-cached`", then return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
    2. Let forwardResponse be the result of running [HTTP-network fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-http-network-fetch) given httpFetchParams, includeCredentials, and isNewConnectionFetch. 
    3. If httpRequest’s [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method) is [unsafe](https://httpwg.org/specs/rfc9110.html#rfc.section.9.2.1) and forwardResponse’s [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status) is in the range 200 to 399, inclusive, invalidate appropriate stored responses in httpCache, as per the "[Invalidating Stored Responses](https://httpwg.org/specs/rfc9111.html#invalidation)" chapter of HTTP Caching, and set storedResponse to null. [[HTTP-CACHING]](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#biblio-http-caching "HTTP Caching")
    4. If the revalidatingFlag is set and forwardResponse’s [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status) is 304, then: 
      1. Update storedResponse’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list) using forwardResponse’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list), as per the "[Freshening Stored Responses upon Validation](https://httpwg.org/specs/rfc9111.html#freshening.responses)" chapter of HTTP Caching. [[HTTP-CACHING]](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#biblio-http-caching "HTTP Caching")
This updates the stored response in cache as well. 
      2. Set response to storedResponse. 
      3. Set response’s [cache state](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-cache-state) to "`validated`". 
    5. If response is null, then: 
      1. Set response to forwardResponse. 
      2. Store httpRequest and forwardResponse in httpCache, as per the "[Storing Responses in Caches](https://httpwg.org/specs/rfc9111.html#response.cacheability)" chapter of HTTP Caching. [[HTTP-CACHING]](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#biblio-http-caching "HTTP Caching")
If forwardResponse is a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error), this effectively caches the network error, which is sometimes known as "negative caching". 
The associated [body info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body-info) is stored in the cache alongside the response. 
  12. Set response’s [URL list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-url-list) to a [clone](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-clone) of httpRequest’s [URL list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url-list). 
  13. If httpRequest’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list) [contains](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#header-list-contains) ``Range``, then set response’s [range-requested flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-range-requested-flag). 
  14. Set response’s [request-includes-credentials](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response-request-includes-credentials) to includeCredentials. 
  15. If response’s [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status) is 401, httpRequest’s [response tainting](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-response-tainting) is not "`cors`", includeCredentials is true, and request’s [traversable for user prompts](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-window) is a [traversable navigable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#traversable-navigable): 
    1. Needs testing: multiple ``WWW-Authenticate`` headers, missing, parsing issues. 
    2. If request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) is non-null, then: 
      1. If request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body)’s [source](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-source) is null, then return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
      2. Set request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) to the [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#body-with-type-body) of the result of [safely extracting](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#bodyinit-safely-extract) request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body)’s [source](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-source). 
    3. If request’s [use-URL-credentials flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-use-url-credentials-flag) is unset or isAuthenticationFetch is true, then: 
      1. If fetchParams is [canceled](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-canceled), then return the [appropriate network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#appropriate-network-error) for fetchParams. 
      2. Let username and password be the result of prompting the end user for a username and password, respectively, in request’s [traversable for user prompts](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-window). 
      3. [Set the username](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#set-the-username) given request’s [current URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-current-url) and username. 
      4. [Set the password](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#set-the-password) given request’s [current URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-current-url) and password. 
    4. Set response to the result of running [HTTP-network-or-cache fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-http-network-or-cache-fetch) given fetchParams and true. 
  16. If response’s [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status) is 407, then: 
    1. If request’s [traversable for user prompts](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-window) is "`no-traversable`", then return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
    2. Needs testing: multiple ``Proxy-Authenticate`` headers, missing, parsing issues. 
    3. If fetchParams is [canceled](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-canceled), then return the [appropriate network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#appropriate-network-error) for fetchParams. 
    4. Prompt the end user as appropriate in request’s [traversable for user prompts](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-window) and store the result as a [proxy-authentication entry](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#proxy-authentication-entry). [[HTTP]](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#biblio-http "HTTP Semantics")
Remaining details surrounding proxy authentication are defined by HTTP. 
    5. Set response to the result of running [HTTP-network-or-cache fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-http-network-or-cache-fetch) given fetchParams. 
  17. If all of the following are true 
     * response’s [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status) is 421 
     * isNewConnectionFetch is false 
     * request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) is null, or request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) is non-null and request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body)’s [source](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-source) is non-null 
then: 
    1. If fetchParams is [canceled](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-canceled), then return the [appropriate network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#appropriate-network-error) for fetchParams. 
    2. Set response to the result of running [HTTP-network-or-cache fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-http-network-or-cache-fetch) given fetchParams, isAuthenticationFetch, and true. 
  18. If isAuthenticationFetch is true, then create an [authentication entry](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#authentication-entry) for request and the given realm. 
  19. Return response. Typically response’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body)’s [stream](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-stream) is still being enqueued to after returning.


###  4.7. HTTP-network fetch
To HTTP-network fetch, given a [fetch params](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params) fetchParams, an optional boolean includeCredentials (default false), and an optional boolean forceNewConnection (default false), run these steps: 
  1. Let request be fetchParams’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-request). 
  2. If request’s [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client) [is offline](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#is-offline), then return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
  3. Let response be null. 
  4. Let timingInfo be fetchParams’s [timing info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-timing-info). 
  5. Let networkPartitionKey be the result of [determining the network partition key](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-determine-the-network-partition-key) given request. 
  6. Let newConnection be "`yes`" if forceNewConnection is true; otherwise "`no`". 
  7. Switch on request’s [mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-mode):  

"`websocket`" 
    
Let connection be the result of [obtaining a WebSocket connection](https://websockets.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-websocket-connection-obtain), given request’s [current URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-current-url).  

"`webtransport`" 
    
Let connection be the result of [obtaining a WebTransport connection](https://w3c.github.io/webtransport/#obtain-a-webtransport-connection), given networkPartitionKey and request.  

Otherwise 
    
Let connection be the result of [obtaining a connection](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-connection-obtain), given networkPartitionKey, request’s [current URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-current-url), includeCredentials, and newConnection. 
  8. Run these steps, but [abort when](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#abort-when) fetchParams is [canceled](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-canceled): 
    1. If connection is failure, then return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
    2. Set timingInfo’s [final connection timing info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-timing-info-final-connection-timing-info) to the result of calling [clamp and coarsen connection timing info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#clamp-and-coarsen-connection-timing-info) with connection’s [timing info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-connection-timing-info), timingInfo’s [post-redirect start time](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-timing-info-post-redirect-start-time), and fetchParams’s [cross-origin isolated capability](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-cross-origin-isolated-capability). 
    3. If connection is an HTTP/1.x connection, request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) is non-null, and request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body)’s [source](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-source) is null, then return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
    4. Set timingInfo’s [final network-request start time](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-timing-info-final-network-request-start-time) to the [coarsened shared current time](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dfn-coarsened-shared-current-time) given fetchParams’s [cross-origin isolated capability](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-cross-origin-isolated-capability). 
    5. Set response to the result of making an HTTP request over connection using request with the following caveats: 
       * Follow the relevant requirements from HTTP. [[HTTP]](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#biblio-http "HTTP Semantics") [[HTTP-CACHING]](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#biblio-http-caching "HTTP Caching")
       * If request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) is non-null, and request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body)’s [source](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-source) is null, then the user agent may have a buffer of up to 64 kibibytes and store a part of request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) in that buffer. If the user agent reads from request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) beyond that buffer’s size and the user agent needs to resend request, then instead return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
The resending is needed when the connection is timed out, for example. 
The buffer is not needed when request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body)’s [source](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-source) is non-null, because request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) can be recreated from it. 
When request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body)’s [source](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-source) is null, it means [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) is created from a `ReadableStream[](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream)` object, which means [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) cannot be recreated and that is why the buffer is needed. 
       * While true: 
         1. Set timingInfo’s [final network-response start time](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-timing-info-final-network-response-start-time) to the [coarsened shared current time](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dfn-coarsened-shared-current-time) given fetchParams’s [cross-origin isolated capability](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-cross-origin-isolated-capability), immediately after the user agent’s HTTP parser receives the first byte of the response (e.g., frame header bytes for HTTP/2 or response status line for HTTP/1.x). 
         2. Wait until all the HTTP response headers are transmitted. 
         3. Run the [WebDriver BiDi response started](https://w3c.github.io/webdriver-bidi/#webdriver-bidi-response-started) steps with request and response. 
         4. Let status be the HTTP response’s status code. 
         5. If status is in the range 100 to 199, inclusive: 
           1. If timingInfo’s [first interim network-response start time](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-timing-info-first-interim-network-response-start-time) is 0, then set timingInfo’s [first interim network-response start time](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-timing-info-first-interim-network-response-start-time) to timingInfo’s [final network-response start time](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-timing-info-final-network-response-start-time). 
           2. If request’s [mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-mode) is "`websocket`" and status is 101, then [break](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-break). 
           3. If status is 103 and fetchParams’s [process early hints response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-process-early-hints-response) is non-null, then [queue a fetch task](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#queue-a-fetch-task) to run fetchParams’s [process early hints response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-process-early-hints-response), with [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response). 
           4. [Continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue). 
These kind of HTTP responses are eventually followed by a "final" HTTP response. 
         6. [Break](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-break). 
The exact layering between Fetch and HTTP still needs to be sorted through and therefore response represents both a [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response) and an HTTP response here. 
If the HTTP request results in a TLS client certificate dialog, then: 
      1. If request’s [traversable for user prompts](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-window) is a [traversable navigable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#traversable-navigable), then make the dialog available in request’s [traversable for user prompts](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-window). 
      2. Otherwise, return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
To transmit request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) body, run these steps: 
      1. If body is null and fetchParams’s [process request end-of-body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-process-request-end-of-body) is non-null, then [queue a fetch task](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#queue-a-fetch-task) given fetchParams’s [process request end-of-body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-process-request-end-of-body) and fetchParams’s [task destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-task-destination). 
      2. Otherwise, if body is non-null: 
        1. Let processBodyChunk given bytes be these steps: 
          1. If fetchParams is [canceled](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-canceled), then abort these steps. 
          2. Run this step [in parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel): transmit bytes. 
          3. If fetchParams’s [process request body chunk length](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-process-request-body) is non-null, then run fetchParams’s [process request body chunk length](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-process-request-body) given bytes’s [length](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-sequence-length). 
        2. Let processEndOfBody be these steps: 
          1. If fetchParams is [canceled](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-canceled), then abort these steps. 
          2. If fetchParams’s [process request end-of-body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-process-request-end-of-body) is non-null, then run fetchParams’s [process request end-of-body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-process-request-end-of-body). 
        3. Let processBodyError given e be these steps:
