          1. If fetchParams is [canceled](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-canceled), then abort these steps. 
          2. If e is an "`AbortError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#aborterror)`" `DOMException[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException)`, then [abort](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-abort) fetchParams’s [controller](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-controller). 
          3. Otherwise, [terminate](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-terminate) fetchParams’s [controller](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-controller). 
        4. [Incrementally read](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#body-incrementally-read) request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) given processBodyChunk, processEndOfBody, processBodyError, and fetchParams’s [task destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-task-destination). 
  9. [If aborted](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#if-aborted), then: 
    1. If connection uses HTTP/2, then transmit an `RST_STREAM` frame. 
    2. Return the [appropriate network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#appropriate-network-error) for fetchParams. 
  10. Let buffer be an empty [byte sequence](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-sequence). 
This represents an internal buffer inside the network layer of the user agent. 
  11. Let stream be a [new](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#new) `ReadableStream[](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream)`. 
  12. Let pullAlgorithm be the following steps: 
    1. Let promise be [a new promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise). 
    2. Run the following steps [in parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel): 
      1. If the size of buffer is smaller than a lower limit chosen by the user agent and the ongoing fetch is [suspended](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch-suspend), [resume](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch-resume) the fetch. 
      2. Wait until buffer is not empty. 
      3. [Queue a fetch task](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#queue-a-fetch-task) to run the following steps, with fetchParams’s [task destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-task-destination). 
        1. [Pull from bytes](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-pull-from-bytes) buffer into stream. 
        2. If stream is [errored](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-errored), then [terminate](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-terminate) fetchParams’s [controller](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-controller). 
        3. [Resolve](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#resolve) promise with undefined. 
    3. Return promise. 
  13. Let cancelAlgorithm be an algorithm that [aborts](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-abort) fetchParams’s [controller](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-controller) with reason, given reason. 
  14. [Set up](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-set-up-with-byte-reading-support) stream with byte reading support with [pullAlgorithm](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-set-up-pullalgorithm) set to pullAlgorithm, [cancelAlgorithm](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-set-up-cancelalgorithm) set to cancelAlgorithm. 
  15. Set response’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body) to a new [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body) whose [stream](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-stream) is stream. 
  16. Run the [WebDriver BiDi clone network response body](https://w3c.github.io/webdriver-bidi/#webdriver-bidi-clone-network-response-body) steps with request and response. 
  17. If includeCredentials is true, then the user agent should [parse and store response ``Set-Cookie`` headers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#parse-and-store-response-set-cookie-headers) given request and response. 
  18. Run these steps [in parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel): 
    1. Run these steps, but [abort when](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#abort-when) fetchParams is [canceled](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-canceled): 
      1. While true: 
        1. If one or more bytes have been transmitted from response’s message body, then: 
          1. Let bytes be the transmitted bytes. 
          2. Let codings be the result of [extracting header list values](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#extract-header-list-values) given ``Content-Encoding`` and response’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list). 
          3. Let filteredCoding be "`@unknown`". 
          4. If codings is null or failure, then set filteredCoding to the empty string. 
          5. Otherwise, if codings’s [size](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-size) is greater than 1, then set filteredCoding to "`multiple`". 
          6. Otherwise, if codings[0] is the empty string, or it is supported by the user agent, and is a [byte-case-insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-case-insensitive) match for an entry listed in the HTTP Content Coding Registry, then set filteredCoding to the result of [byte-lowercasing](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-lowercase) codings[0]. [[IANA-HTTP-PARAMS]](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#biblio-iana-http-params "Hypertext Transfer Protocol \(HTTP\) Parameters")
          7. Set response’s [body info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body-info)’s [content encoding](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response-body-info-content-encoding) to filteredCoding. 
          8. Increase response’s [body info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body-info)’s [encoded size](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-timing-info-encoded-body-size) by bytes’s [length](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-sequence-length). 
          9. Set bytes to the result of [handling content codings](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#handle-content-codings) given codings and bytes. 
This makes the ``Content-Length`` [header](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header) unreliable to the extent that it was reliable to begin with. 
          10. Increase response’s [body info](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body-info)’s [decoded size](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-timing-info-decoded-body-size) by bytes’s [length](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-sequence-length). 
          11. If bytes is failure, then [terminate](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-terminate) fetchParams’s [controller](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-controller). 
          12. Append bytes to buffer. 
          13. If the size of buffer is larger than an upper limit chosen by the user agent, ask the user agent to [suspend](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch-suspend) the ongoing fetch. 
        2. Otherwise, if the bytes transmission for response’s message body is done normally and stream is [readable](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-readable), then [close](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-close) stream, and abort these in-parallel steps. 
    2. [If aborted](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#if-aborted), then: 
      1. If fetchParams is [aborted](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-aborted), then: 
        1. Set response’s [aborted flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-aborted). 
        2. If stream is [readable](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-readable), then [error](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-error) stream with the result of [deserialize a serialized abort reason](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#deserialize-a-serialized-abort-reason) given fetchParams’s [controller](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-controller)’s [serialized abort reason](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-serialized-abort-reason) and an [implementation-defined](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#implementation-defined) [realm](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#realm). 
      2. Otherwise, if stream is [readable](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-readable), [error](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-error) stream with a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
      3. If connection uses HTTP/2, then transmit an `RST_STREAM` frame. 
      4. Otherwise, the user agent should close connection unless it would be bad for performance to do so. 
For instance, the user agent could keep the connection open if it knows there’s only a few bytes of transfer remaining on a reusable connection. In this case it could be worse to close the connection and go through the handshake process again for the next fetch. 
These are run [in parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel) as at this point it is unclear whether response’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body) is relevant (response might be a redirect). 
  19. Return response. Typically response’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body)’s [stream](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-stream) is still being enqueued to after returning.


###  4.8. CORS-preflight fetch
This is effectively the user agent implementation of the check to see if the [CORS protocol](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cors-protocol) is understood. The so-called [CORS-preflight request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cors-preflight-request). If successful it populates the [CORS-preflight cache](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache) to minimize the number of these [fetches](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cors-preflight-fetch-0). 
To CORS-preflight fetch, given a [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request) request, run these steps: 
  1. Let preflight be a new [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request) whose [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method) is ``OPTIONS``, [URL list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url-list) is a [clone](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-clone) of request’s [URL list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url-list), [initiator](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-initiator) is request’s [initiator](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-initiator), [destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-destination) is request’s [destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-destination), [origin](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-origin) is request’s [origin](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-origin), [referrer](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-referrer) is request’s [referrer](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-referrer), [referrer policy](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-referrer-policy) is request’s [referrer policy](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-referrer-policy), [mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-mode) is "`cors`", [response tainting](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-response-tainting) is "`cors`", and [WebDriver id](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-webdriver-id) is request’s [WebDriver id](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-webdriver-id). 
The [service-workers mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-service-workers-mode) of preflight does not matter as this algorithm uses [HTTP-network-or-cache fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-http-network-or-cache-fetch) rather than [HTTP fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-http-fetch). 
  2. [Append](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-append) (``Accept``, ``*/*``) to preflight’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list). 
  3. [Append](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-append) (`[`Access-Control-Request-Method`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#http-access-control-request-method)`, request’s [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method)) to preflight’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list). 
  4. Let headers be the [CORS-unsafe request-header names](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cors-unsafe-request-header-names) with request’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list). 
  5. If headers [is not empty](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-is-empty), then: 
    1. Let value be the items in headers separated from each other by ``,``. 
    2. [Append](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-append) (`[`Access-Control-Request-Headers`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#http-access-control-request-headers)`, value) to preflight’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list). 
This intentionally does not use [combine](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-combine), as 0x20 following 0x2C is not the way this was implemented, for better or worse. 
  6. Let response be the result of running [HTTP-network-or-cache fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-http-network-or-cache-fetch) given a new [fetch params](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params) whose [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-params-request) is preflight. 
  7. If a [CORS check](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cors-check) for request and response returns success and response’s [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status) is an [ok status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#ok-status), then: 
The [CORS check](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cors-check) is done on request rather than preflight to ensure the correct [credentials mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-credentials-mode) is used. 
    1. Let methods be the result of [extracting header list values](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#extract-header-list-values) given `[`Access-Control-Allow-Methods`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#http-access-control-allow-methods)` and response’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list). 
    2. Let headerNames be the result of [extracting header list values](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#extract-header-list-values) given `[`Access-Control-Allow-Headers`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#http-access-control-allow-headers)` and response’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list). 
    3. If either methods or headerNames is failure, return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
    4. If methods is null and request’s [use-CORS-preflight flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#use-cors-preflight-flag) is set, then set methods to a new list containing request’s [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method). 
This ensures that a [CORS-preflight fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cors-preflight-fetch-0) that happened due to request’s [use-CORS-preflight flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#use-cors-preflight-flag) being set is [cached](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache). 
    5. If request’s [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method) is not in methods, request’s [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method) is not a [CORS-safelisted method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cors-safelisted-method), and request’s [credentials mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-credentials-mode) is "`include`" or methods does not contain ``*``, then return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
    6. If one of request’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list)’s [names](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-name) is a [CORS non-wildcard request-header name](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cors-non-wildcard-request-header-name) and is not a [byte-case-insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-case-insensitive) match for an [item](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item) in headerNames, then return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
    7. [For each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) unsafeName of the [CORS-unsafe request-header names](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cors-unsafe-request-header-names) with request’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list), if unsafeName is not a [byte-case-insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-case-insensitive) match for an [item](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-item) in headerNames and request’s [credentials mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-credentials-mode) is "`include`" or headerNames does not contain ``*``, return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 
    8. Let max-age be the result of [extracting header list values](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#extract-header-list-values) given `[`Access-Control-Max-Age`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#http-access-control-max-age)` and response’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list). 
    9. If max-age is failure or null, then set max-age to 5. 
    10. If max-age is greater than an imposed limit on [max-age](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-max-age), then set max-age to the imposed limit. 
    11. If the user agent does not provide for a [cache](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache), then return response. 
    12. For each method in methods for which there is a [method cache entry match](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-match-method) using request, set matching entry’s [max-age](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-max-age) to max-age. 
    13. For each method in methods for which there is no [method cache entry match](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-match-method) using request, [create a new cache entry](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-create-entry) with request, max-age, method, and null. 
    14. For each headerName in headerNames for which there is a [header-name cache entry match](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-match-header) using request, set matching entry’s [max-age](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-max-age) to max-age. 
    15. For each headerName in headerNames for which there is no [header-name cache entry match](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-match-header) using request, [create a new cache entry](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-create-entry) with request, max-age, null, and headerName. 
    16. Return response. 
  8. Otherwise, return a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error). 


###  4.9. CORS-preflight cache
A user agent has an associated [CORS-preflight cache](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache). A CORS-preflight cache is a [list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list) of [cache entries](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cache-entry). 
A cache entry consists of: 
  * key (a [network partition key](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#network-partition-key)) 
  * byte-serialized origin (a [byte sequence](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-sequence)) 
  * URL (a [URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url)) 
  * max-age (a number of seconds) 
  * credentials (a boolean) 
  * method (null, ``*``, or a [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-method)) 
  * header name (null, ``*``, or a [header name](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#header-name)) 


[Cache entries](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cache-entry) must be removed after the seconds specified in their [max-age](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-max-age) field have passed since storing the entry. [Cache entries](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cache-entry) may be removed before that moment arrives. 
To create a new cache entry, given request, max-age, method, and headerName, run these steps: 
  1. Let entry be a [cache entry](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cache-entry), initialized as follows:  

[key](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-key) 
    
The result of [determining the network partition key](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-determine-the-network-partition-key) given request 

[byte-serialized origin](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-origin) 
    
The result of [byte-serializing a request origin](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-serializing-a-request-origin) with request 

[URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-url) 
    
request’s [current URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-current-url) 

[max-age](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-max-age) 
    
max-age 

[credentials](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-credentials) 
    
True if request’s [credentials mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-credentials-mode) is "`include`", and false otherwise  

[method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-method) 
    
method 

[header name](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-header-name) 
    
headerName
  2. [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append) entry to the user agent’s [CORS-preflight cache](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache). 


To clear cache entries, given a request, [remove](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-remove) any [cache entries](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cache-entry) in the user agent’s [CORS-preflight cache](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache) whose [key](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-key) is the result of [determining the network partition key](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-determine-the-network-partition-key) given request, [byte-serialized origin](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-origin) is the result of [byte-serializing a request origin](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-serializing-a-request-origin) with request, and [URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-url) is request’s [current URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-current-url). 
There is a cache entry match for a [cache entry](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cache-entry) entry with request if entry’s [key](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-key) is the result of [determining the network partition key](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-determine-the-network-partition-key) given request, entry’s [byte-serialized origin](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-origin) is the result of [byte-serializing a request origin](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-serializing-a-request-origin) with request, entry’s [URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-url) is request’s [current URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-current-url), and one of 
  * entry’s [credentials](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-credentials) is true 
  * entry’s [credentials](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-credentials) is false and request’s [credentials mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-credentials-mode) is not "`include`". 


is true. 
There is a method cache entry match for method using request when there is a [cache entry](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cache-entry) in the user agent’s [CORS-preflight cache](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache) for which there is a [cache entry match](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-match) with request and its [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-method) is method or ``*``. 
There is a header-name cache entry match for headerName using request when there is a [cache entry](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cache-entry) in the user agent’s [CORS-preflight cache](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache) for which there is a [cache entry match](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-match) with request and one of 
  * its [header name](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-header-name) is a [byte-case-insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-case-insensitive) match for headerName
  * its [header name](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-cache-header-name) is ``*`` and headerName is not a [CORS non-wildcard request-header name](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cors-non-wildcard-request-header-name)


is true. 
###  4.10. CORS check
To perform a CORS check for a request and response, run these steps: 
  1. Let origin be the result of [getting](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-get) `[`Access-Control-Allow-Origin`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#http-access-control-allow-origin)` from response’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list). 
  2. If origin is null, then return failure. 
Null is not ``null``. 
  3. If request’s [credentials mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-credentials-mode) is not "`include`" and origin is ``*``, then return success. 
  4. If the result of [byte-serializing a request origin](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-serializing-a-request-origin) with request is not origin, then return failure. 
  5. If request’s [credentials mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-credentials-mode) is not "`include`", then return success. 
  6. Let credentials be the result of [getting](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-get) `[`Access-Control-Allow-Credentials`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#http-access-control-allow-credentials)` from response’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list). 
  7. If credentials is ``true``, then return success. 
  8. Return failure. 


###  4.11. TAO check
To perform a TAO check for a request and response, run these steps: 
  1. [Assert](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert): request’s [origin](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-origin) is not "`client`". 
  2. If request’s [timing allow failed flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#timing-allow-failed) is set, then return failure. 
  3. Let values be the result of [getting, decoding, and splitting](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-get-decode-split) ``Timing-Allow-Origin`` from response’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list). 
  4. If values [contains](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-contain) "`*`", then return success. 
  5. If values [contains](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-contain) the result of [serializing a request origin](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#serializing-a-request-origin) with request, then return success. 
  6. If request’s [mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-mode) is "`navigate`" and request’s [current URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-current-url)’s [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin) is not [same origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#same-origin) with request’s [origin](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-origin), then return failure. 
This is necessary for navigations of a nested navigable. There, request’s [origin](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-origin) would be the container document’s [origin](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document-origin) and the [TAO check](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-tao-check) would return failure. Since navigation timing never validates the results of the [TAO check](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-tao-check), the nested document would still have access to the full timing information, but the container document would not. 
  7. If request’s [response tainting](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-response-tainting) is "`basic`", then return success. 
  8. Return failure. 


To append to a request’s navigation timing allow values list, given a [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request) request and a [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response) response: 
  1. [Assert](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert): request is a [navigation request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#navigation-request). 
  2. Let taoValues be the result of [getting, decoding, and splitting](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-get-decode-split) ``Timing-Allow-Origin`` from response’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list). 
  3. If taoValues is null, then set taoValues to « ». 
  4. [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append) taoValues to request’s [navigation timing allow values list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-navigation-timing-allow-values-list). 


To perform a navigation TAO check for a [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response) response and an [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin) destinationOrigin: 
  1. [For each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) taoValues of response’s [navigation timing allow values list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response-navigation-timing-allow-values-list): 
    1. If taoValues [contains](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-contain) "`*`", then [continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue). 
    2. If taoValues [contains](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-contain) destinationOrigin, [serialized](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#ascii-serialisation-of-an-origin), then [continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue). 
    3. Return [failure](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#failure). 
  2. Return [success](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#success). 


###  4.12. Deferred fetching
Deferred fetching allows callers to request that a fetch is invoked at the latest possible moment, i.e., when a [fetch group](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch-group) is [terminated](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch-group-terminate), or after a timeout. 
The deferred fetch task source is a [task source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-source) used to update the result of a deferred fetch. User agents must prioritize tasks in this [task source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-source) before other task sources, specifically task sources that can result in running scripts such as the [DOM manipulation task source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#dom-manipulation-task-source), to reflect the most recent state of a [`fetchLater()`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-window-fetchlater) call before running any scripts that might depend on it. 
To queue a deferred fetch given a [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request) request, a null or `DOMHighResTimeStamp[](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp)` activateAfter, and onActivatedWithoutTermination, which is an algorithm that takes no arguments: 
  1. [Populate request from client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#populate-request-from-client) given request. 
  2. Set request’s [service-workers mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-service-workers-mode) to "`none`". 
  3. Set request’s [keepalive](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-keepalive-flag) to true. 
  4. Let deferredRecord be a new [deferred fetch record](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#deferred-fetch-record) whose [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#deferred-fetch-record-request) is request, and whose [notify invoked](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#deferred-fetch-record-notify-invoked) is onActivatedWithoutTermination. 
  5. [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append) deferredRecord to request’s [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client)’s [fetch group](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#environment-settings-object-fetch-group)’s [deferred fetch records](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-group-deferred-fetch-records). 
  6. If activateAfter is non-null, then run the following steps [in parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel): 
    1. The user agent should wait until any of the following conditions is met: 
       * At least activateAfter milliseconds have passed. 
       * The user agent has a reason to believe that it is about to lose the opportunity to execute scripts, e.g., when the browser is moved to the background, or when request’s [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client)’s [global object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global) is a `Window[](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window)` object whose [associated document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#concept-document-window) had a "`hidden`" [visibility state](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/interaction.html#visibility-state) for a long period of time. 
    2. [Process](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#process-a-deferred-fetch) deferredRecord. 
  7. Return deferredRecord. 


To compute the total request length of a [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request) request: 
  1. Let totalRequestLength be the [length](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string-length) of request’s [URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url), [serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer) with [_exclude fragment_](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#url-serializer-exclude-fragment) set to true. 
  2. Increment totalRequestLength by the [length](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string-length) of request’s [referrer](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-referrer), [serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer). 
  3. [For each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) (name, value) of request’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list), increment totalRequestLength by name’s [length](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-sequence-length) + value’s [length](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-sequence-length). 
  4. Increment totalRequestLength by request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body)’s [length](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-total-bytes). 
  5. Return totalRequestLength. 


To process deferred fetches given a [fetch group](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch-group) fetchGroup: 
  1. [For each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) [deferred fetch record](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-group-deferred-fetch-records) deferredRecord of fetchGroup’s [deferred fetch records](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-group-deferred-fetch-records), [process a deferred fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#process-a-deferred-fetch) deferredRecord. 


To process a deferred fetch deferredRecord: 
  1. If deferredRecord’s [invoke state](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#deferred-fetch-record-invoke-state) is not "`pending`", then return. 
  2. Set deferredRecord’s [invoke state](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#deferred-fetch-record-invoke-state) to "`sent`". 
  3. [Fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch) deferredRecord’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#deferred-fetch-record-request). 
  4. [Queue a global task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-global-task) on the [deferred fetch task source](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#deferred-fetch-task-source) with deferredRecord’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#deferred-fetch-record-request)’s [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client)’s [global object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global) to run deferredRecord’s [notify invoked](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#deferred-fetch-record-notify-invoked). 


####  4.12.1. Deferred fetching quota
_This section is non-normative._
The deferred-fetch quota is allocated to a [top-level traversable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#top-level-traversable) (a "tab"), amounting to 640 kibibytes. The top-level document and its same-origin directly nested documents can use this quota to queue deferred fetches, or delegate some of it to cross-origin nested documents, using permissions policy. 
By default, 128 kibibytes out of these 640 kibibytes are allocated to delegating the quota to cross-origin nested documents, each reserving 8 kibibytes. 
The top-level [document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document), and subsequently its nested documents, can control how much of their quota is delegated to cross-origin child documents, using permissions policy. By default, the "`deferred-fetch-minimal[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-permissionspolicy-deferred-fetch-minimal)`" policy is enabled for any origin, while "`deferred-fetch[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-permissionspolicy-deferred-fetch)`" is enabled for the top-level document’s origin only. By relaxing the "`deferred-fetch[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-permissionspolicy-deferred-fetch)`" policy for particular origins and nested documents, the top-level document can allocate 64 kibibytes to those nested documents. Similarly, by restricting the "`deferred-fetch-minimal[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-permissionspolicy-deferred-fetch-minimal)`" policy for a particular origin or nested document, the document can prevent the document from reserving the 8 kibibytes it would receive by default. By disabling the "`deferred-fetch-minimal[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-permissionspolicy-deferred-fetch-minimal)`" policy for the top-level document itself, the entire 128 kibibytes delegated quota is collected back into the main pool of 640 kibibytes. 
Out of the allocated quota for a [document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document), only 64 kibibytes can be used concurrently for the same reporting origin (the [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request)’s [URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url)’s [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin)). This prevents a situation where particular third-party libraries would reserve quota opportunistically, before they have data to send. 
Any of the following calls to [`fetchLater()`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-window-fetchlater) would throw due to the request itself exceeding the 64 kibibytes quota allocated to a reporting origin. Note that the size of the request includes the [URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url) itself, the [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body), the [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list), and the [referrer](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-referrer). 

```
fetchLater(a_72_kb_url);
fetchLater("https://origin.example.com", {headers: headers_exceeding_64kb});
fetchLater(a_32_kb_url, {headers: headers_exceeding_32kb});
fetchLater("https://origin.example.com", {method: "POST", body: body_exceeding_64_kb});
fetchLater(a_62_kb_url /* with a 3kb referrer */);

```

In the following sequence, the first two requests would succeed, but the third one would throw. That’s because the overall 640 kibibytes quota was not exceeded in the first two calls, however the 3rd request exceeds the reporting-origin quota for `https://a.example.com`, and would throw. 

```
fetchLater("https://a.example.com", {method: "POST", body: a_64kb_body});
fetchLater("https://b.example.com", {method: "POST", body: a_64kb_body});
fetchLater("https://a.example.com");

```

Same-origin nested documents share the quota of their parent. However, cross-origin or cross-agent iframes only receive 8kb of quota by default. So in the following example, the first three calls would succeed and the last one would throw. 

```
// In main page
fetchLater("https://a.example.com", {method: "POST", body: a_64kb_body});

// In same-origin nested document
fetchLater("https://b.example.com", {method: "POST", body: a_64kb_body});

// In cross-origin nested document at https://fratop.example.com
fetchLater("https://a.example.com", {body: a_5kb_body});
fetchLater("https://a.example.com", {body: a_12kb_body});

```

To make the previous example not throw, the top-level document can delegate some of its quota to `https://fratop.example.com`, for example by serving the following header: 

```
Permissions-Policy: deferred-fetch=(self "https://fratop.example.com")
```

Each nested document reserves its own quota. So the following would work, because each frame reserves 8 kibibytes: 

```
// In cross-origin nested document at https://fratop.example.com/frame-1
fetchLater("https://a.example.com", {body: a_6kb_body});

// In cross-origin nested document at https://fratop.example.com/frame-2
fetchLater("https://a.example.com", {body: a_6kb_body});

```

The following tree illustrates how quota is distributed to different nested documents in a tree: 
  * `https://top.example.com`, with permissions policy set to `Permissions-policy: deferred-fetch=(self "https://ok.example.com")`
    * `https://top.example.com/frame`: shares quota with the top-level traversable, as they are same origin. 
      * `https://x.example.com`: receives 8 kibibytes.
    * `https://x.example.com`: receives 8 kibibytes. 
      * `https://top.example.com`: 0. Even though it’s same origin with the top-level traversable, it does not automatically share its quota as they are separated by a cross-origin intermediary.
    * `https://ok.example.com/good`: receives 64 kibibytes, granted via the "`deferred-fetch[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-permissionspolicy-deferred-fetch)`" policy. 
      * `https://x.example.com`: receives no quota. Only documents with the same origin as the top-level traversable can grant the 8 kibibytes based on the "`deferred-fetch-minimal[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-permissionspolicy-deferred-fetch-minimal)`" policy.
    * `https://ok.example.com/redirect`, navigated to `https://x.example.com`: receives no quota. The reserved 64 kibibytes for `https://ok.example.com` are not available for `https://x.example.com`. 
    * `https://ok.example.com/back`, navigated to `https://top.example.com`: shares quota with the top-level traversable, as they’re same origin. 


In the above example, the [top-level traversable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#top-level-traversable) and its [same origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#same-origin) descendants share a quota of 384 kibibytes. That value is computed as such: 
  * 640 kibibytes are initially granted to the [top-level traversable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#top-level-traversable). 
  * 128 kibibytes are reserved for the "`deferred-fetch-minimal[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-permissionspolicy-deferred-fetch-minimal)`" policy. 
  * 64 kibibytes are reserved for the container navigating to `https://ok.example/good`. 
  * 64 kibibytes are reserved for the container navigating to `https://ok.example/redirect`, and lost when it navigates away. 
  * `https://ok.example.com/back` did not reserve 64 kibibytes, because it navigated back to [top-level traversable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#top-level-traversable)’s origin. 
  * 640 − 128 − 64 − 64 = 384 kibibytes. 


This specification defines a [policy-controlled feature](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-permissions-policy/#policy-controlled-feature) identified by the string "`deferred-fetch`". Its [default allowlist](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-permissions-policy/#policy-controlled-feature-default-allowlist) is "`self`". 
This specification defines a [policy-controlled feature](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-permissions-policy/#policy-controlled-feature) identified by the string "`deferred-fetch-minimal`". Its [default allowlist](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-permissions-policy/#policy-controlled-feature-default-allowlist) is "`*`". 
The quota reserved for `deferred-fetch-minimal` is 128 kibibytes. 
Each [navigable container](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#navigable-container) has an associated number reserved deferred-fetch quota. Its possible values are minimal quota, which is 8 kibibytes, and normal quota, which is 0 or 64 kibibytes. Unless stated otherwise, it is 0. 
To get the available deferred-fetch quota given a [document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document) document and an [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin)-or-null origin: 
  1. Let controlDocument be document’s [deferred-fetch control document](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#deferred-fetch-control-document). 
  2. Let navigable be controlDocument’s [node navigable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#node-navigable). 
  3. Let isTopLevel be true if controlDocument’s [node navigable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#node-navigable) is a [top-level traversable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#top-level-traversable); otherwise false. 
  4. Let deferredFetchAllowed be true if controlDocument is [allowed to use](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/iframe-embed-object.html#allowed-to-use) the [policy-controlled feature](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-permissions-policy/#policy-controlled-feature) "`deferred-fetch[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-permissionspolicy-deferred-fetch)`"; otherwise false. 
  5. Let deferredFetchMinimalAllowed be true if controlDocument is [allowed to use](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/iframe-embed-object.html#allowed-to-use) the [policy-controlled feature](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-permissions-policy/#policy-controlled-feature) "`deferred-fetch-minimal[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-permissionspolicy-deferred-fetch-minimal)`"; otherwise false. 
  6. Let quota be the result of the first matching statement:  

isTopLevel is true and deferredFetchAllowed is false 
    0  

isTopLevel is true and deferredFetchMinimalAllowed is false 
    
640 kibibytes 
640kb should be enough for everyone.  

isTopLevel is true 
    
512 kibibytes 
The default of 640 kibibytes, decremented by [quota reserved for `deferred-fetch-minimal`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#quota-reserved-for-deferred-fetch-minimal).  

deferredFetchAllowed is true, and navigable’s [navigable container](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#navigable-container)’s [reserved deferred-fetch quota](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#reserved-deferred-fetch-quota) is [normal quota](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#reserved-deferred-fetch-quota-normal-quota) 
     [normal quota](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#reserved-deferred-fetch-quota-normal-quota) 

deferredFetchMinimalAllowed is true, and navigable’s [navigable container](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#navigable-container)’s [reserved deferred-fetch quota](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#reserved-deferred-fetch-quota) is [minimal quota](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#reserved-deferred-fetch-quota-minimal-quota) 
     [minimal quota](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#reserved-deferred-fetch-quota-minimal-quota) 

Otherwise 
    0 
  7. Let quotaForRequestOrigin be 64 kibibytes. 
  8. [For each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) navigable in controlDocument’s [node navigable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#node-navigable)’s [inclusive descendant navigables](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#inclusive-descendant-navigables) whose [active document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document)’s [deferred-fetch control document](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#deferred-fetch-control-document) is controlDocument: 
    1. [For each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) container in navigable’s [active document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document)’s [shadow-including inclusive descendants](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-shadow-including-inclusive-descendant) which is a [navigable container](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#navigable-container), decrement quota by container’s [reserved deferred-fetch quota](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#reserved-deferred-fetch-quota). 
    2. [For each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) [deferred fetch record](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#deferred-fetch-record) deferredRecord of navigable’s [active document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-document)’s [relevant settings object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object)’s [fetch group](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#environment-settings-object-fetch-group)’s [deferred fetch records](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-group-deferred-fetch-records): 
      1. If deferredRecord’s [invoke state](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#deferred-fetch-record-invoke-state) is not "`pending`", then [continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue). 
      2. Let requestLength be the [total request length](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#total-request-length) of deferredRecord’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#deferred-fetch-record-request). 
      3. Decrement quota by requestLength. 
      4. If deferredRecord’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#deferred-fetch-record-request)’s [URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url)’s [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin) is [same origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#same-origin) with origin, then decrement quotaForRequestOrigin by requestLength. 
  9. If quota is equal or less than 0, then return 0. 
  10. If quota is less than quotaForRequestOrigin, then return quota. 
  11. Return quotaForRequestOrigin. 


To reserve deferred-fetch quota for a [navigable container](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#navigable-container) container given an [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin) originToNavigateTo: 
This is called on navigation, when the source document of the navigation is the [navigable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#navigable)’s parent document. It potentially reserves either 64kb or 8kb of quota for the container and its navigable, if allowed by permissions policy. It is not observable to the container document whether the reserved quota was used in practice. This algorithm assumes that the container’s document might delegate quota to the navigated container, and the reserved quota would only apply in that case, and would be ignored if it ends up being shared. If quota was reserved and the document ends up being [same origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#same-origin) with its parent, the quota would be [freed](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#potentially-free-deferred-fetch-quota). 
  1. Set container’s [reserved deferred-fetch quota](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#reserved-deferred-fetch-quota) to 0. 
  2. Let controlDocument be container’s [node document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-node-document)’s [deferred-fetch control document](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#deferred-fetch-control-document). 
  3. If the [inherited policy](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-permissions-policy/#algo-define-inherited-policy-in-container) for "`deferred-fetch[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-permissionspolicy-deferred-fetch)`", container and originToNavigateTo is `"Enabled"`, and the [available deferred-fetch quota](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#available-deferred-fetch-quota) for controlDocument is equal or greater than [normal quota](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#reserved-deferred-fetch-quota-normal-quota), then set container’s [reserved deferred-fetch quota](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#reserved-deferred-fetch-quota) to [normal quota](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#reserved-deferred-fetch-quota-normal-quota) and return. 
  4. If all of the following conditions are true: 
     * controlDocument’s [node navigable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#node-navigable) is a [top-level traversable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#top-level-traversable); 
     * the [inherited policy](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-permissions-policy/#algo-define-inherited-policy-in-container) for "`deferred-fetch-minimal[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-permissionspolicy-deferred-fetch-minimal)`", container and originToNavigateTo is `"Enabled"`; and 
     * the [size](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-size) of controlDocument’s [node navigable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#node-navigable)’s [descendant navigables](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#descendant-navigables), [removing](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-remove) any [navigable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#navigable) whose [navigable container](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#navigable-container)’s [reserved deferred-fetch quota](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#reserved-deferred-fetch-quota) is not [minimal quota](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#reserved-deferred-fetch-quota-minimal-quota), is less than [quota reserved for `deferred-fetch-minimal`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#quota-reserved-for-deferred-fetch-minimal) / [minimal quota](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#reserved-deferred-fetch-quota-minimal-quota), 
then set container’s [reserved deferred-fetch quota](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#reserved-deferred-fetch-quota) to [minimal quota](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#reserved-deferred-fetch-quota-minimal-quota). 


To potentially free deferred-fetch quota for a [document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document) document, if document’s [node navigable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#node-navigable)’s [container document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-container-document) is not null, and its [origin](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document-origin) is [same origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#same-origin) with document, then set document’s [node navigable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#node-navigable)’s [navigable container](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#navigable-container)’s [reserved deferred-fetch quota](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#reserved-deferred-fetch-quota) to 0. 
This is called when a [document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document) is created. It ensures that same-origin nested documents don’t reserve quota, as they anyway share their parent quota. It can only be called upon document creation, as the [origin](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document-origin) of the [document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document) is only known after redirects are handled. 
To get the deferred-fetch control document of a [document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document) document: 
  1. If document’ [node navigable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#node-navigable)’s [container document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-container-document) is null or a [document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document) whose [origin](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document-origin) is not [same origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#same-origin) with document, then return document; otherwise, return the [deferred-fetch control document](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#deferred-fetch-control-document) given document’s [node navigable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#node-navigable)’s [container document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/document-sequences.html#nav-container-document). 


##  5. Fetch API
The [`fetch()`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-global-fetch) method is relatively low-level API for [fetching](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch) resources. It covers slightly more ground than `XMLHttpRequest[](https://xhr.spec.whatwg.org/#xmlhttprequest)`, although it is currently lacking when it comes to request progression (not response progression). 
The [`fetch()`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-global-fetch) method makes it quite straightforward to [fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch) a resource and extract its contents as a `Blob[](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#dfn-Blob)`: 

```
fetch("/music/pk/altes-kamuffel.flac")
  .then(res => res.blob()).then(playBlob)

```

If you just care to log a particular response header: 

```
fetch("/", {method:"HEAD"})
  .then(res => log(res.headers.get("strict-transport-security")))

```

If you want to check a particular response header and then process the response of a cross-origin resource: 

```
fetch("https://pk.example/berlin-calling.json", {mode:"cors"})
  .then(res => {
    if(res.headers.get("content-type") &&
       res.headers.get("content-type").toLowerCase().indexOf("application/json") >= 0) {
      return res.json()
    } else {
      throw new TypeError()
    }
  }).then(processJSON)

```

If you want to work with URL query parameters: 

```
var url = new URL("https://geo.example.org/api"),
    params = {lat:35.696233, long:139.570431}
Object.keys(params).forEach(key => url.searchParams.append(key, params[key]))
fetch(url).then(/* … */)

```

If you want to receive the body data progressively: 

```
function consume(reader) {
  var total = 0
  return pump()
  function pump() {
    return reader.read().then(({done, value}) => {
      if (done) {
        return
      }
      total += value.byteLength
      log(`received ${value.byteLength} bytes (${total} bytes in total)`)
      return pump()
    })
  }
}

fetch("/music/pk/altes-kamuffel.flac")
  .then(res => consume(res.body.getReader()))
  .then(() => log("consumed the entire body without keeping the whole thing in memory!"))
  .catch(e => log("something went wrong: " + e))

```

###  5.1. Headers class

```
typedef ([sequence](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-sequence)<[sequence](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-sequence)<[ByteString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-ByteString)>> or [record](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-record)<[ByteString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-ByteString), [ByteString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-ByteString)>) HeadersInit;

[[Exposed](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#Exposed)=(Window,Worker)]
interface Headers {
  [constructor](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-headers)(optional [HeadersInit](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#typedefdef-headersinit) init);

  [undefined](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-undefined) [append](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-headers-append)([ByteString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-ByteString) name, [ByteString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-ByteString) value);
  [undefined](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-undefined) [delete](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-headers-delete)([ByteString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-ByteString) name);
  [ByteString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-ByteString)? [get](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-headers-get)([ByteString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-ByteString) name);
  [sequence](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-sequence)<[ByteString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-ByteString)> [getSetCookie](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-headers-getsetcookie)();
  [boolean](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-boolean) [has](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-headers-has)([ByteString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-ByteString) name);
  [undefined](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-undefined) [set](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-headers-set)([ByteString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-ByteString) name, [ByteString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-ByteString) value);
  [iterable](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-iterable)<[ByteString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-ByteString), [ByteString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-ByteString)>;
};

```

A `Headers[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers)` object has an associated header list (a [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list)), which is initially empty. This can be a pointer to the [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list) of something else, e.g., of a [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request) as demonstrated by `Request[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request)` objects.
A `Headers[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers)` object also has an associated guard, which is a headers guard. A [headers guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers-guard) is "`immutable`", "`request`", "`request-no-cors`", "`response`" or "`none`". 
* * * 

`headers = new Headers[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-headers)([init])` 
    
Creates a new `Headers[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers)` object. init can be used to fill its internal header list, as per the example below. 

```
const meta = { "Content-Type": "text/xml", "Breaking-Bad": "<3" };
new Headers(meta);

// The above is equivalent to
const meta2 = [
  [ "Content-Type", "text/xml" ],
  [ "Breaking-Bad", "<3" ]
];
new Headers(meta2);

```


`headers . append[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-headers-append)(name, value)` 
    
Appends a header to headers.  

`headers . delete[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-headers-delete)(name)` 
    
Removes a header from headers.  

`headers . get[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-headers-get)(name)` 
    
Returns as a string the values of all headers whose name is name, separated by a comma and a space.  

`headers . getSetCookie[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-headers-getsetcookie)()` 
    
Returns a list of the values for all headers whose name is ``Set-Cookie``.  

`headers . has[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-headers-has)(name)` 
    
Returns whether there is a header whose name is name.  

`headers . set[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-headers-set)(name, value)` 
    
Replaces the value of the first header whose name is name with value and removes any remaining headers whose name is name.  

`for(const [name, value] of headers)` 
    
headers can be iterated over. 
* * *
To validate a [header](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header) (name, value) for a `Headers[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers)` object headers: 
  1. If name is not a [header name](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#header-name) or value is not a [header value](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#header-value), then [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
  2. If headers’s [guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-guard) is "`immutable`", then [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
  3. If headers’s [guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-guard) is "`request`" and (name, value) is a [forbidden request-header](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#forbidden-request-header), then return false. 
  4. If headers’s [guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-guard) is "`response`" and name is a [forbidden response-header name](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#forbidden-response-header-name), then return false. 
  5. Return true. 


Steps for "`request-no-cors`" are not shared as you cannot have a fake value (for `delete()[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-headers-delete)`) that always succeeds in [CORS-safelisted request-header](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cors-safelisted-request-header). 
To append a [header](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header) (name, value) to a `Headers[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers)` object headers, run these steps: 
  1. [Normalize](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-value-normalize) value. 
  2. If [validating](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers-validate) (name, value) for headers returns false, then return. 
  3. If headers’s [guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-guard) is "`request-no-cors`": 
    1. Let temporaryValue be the result of [getting](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-get) name from headers’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-header-list). 
    2. If temporaryValue is null, then set temporaryValue to value. 
    3. Otherwise, set temporaryValue to temporaryValue, followed by 0x2C 0x20, followed by value. 
    4. If (name, temporaryValue) is not a [no-CORS-safelisted request-header](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#no-cors-safelisted-request-header), then return. 
  4. [Append](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-append) (name, value) to headers’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-header-list). 
  5. If headers’s [guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-guard) is "`request-no-cors`", then [remove privileged no-CORS request-headers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-remove-privileged-no-cors-request-headers) from headers. 


To fill a `Headers[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers)` object headers with a given object object, run these steps: 
  1. If object is a [sequence](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-sequence), then [for each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) header of object: 
    1. If header’s [size](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-size) is not 2, then [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
    2. [Append](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-append) (header[0], header[1]) to headers. 
  2. Otherwise, object is a [record](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-list-and-record-specification-type), then [for each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-iterate) key → value of object, [append](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-append) (key, value) to headers. 


To remove privileged no-CORS request-headers from a `Headers[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers)` object (headers), run these steps: 
  1. [For each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) headerName of [privileged no-CORS request-header names](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#privileged-no-cors-request-header-name): 
    1. [Delete](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-delete) headerName from headers’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-header-list). 


This is called when headers are modified by unprivileged code. 
The `new Headers(init)` constructor steps are: 
  1. Set [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-guard) to "`none`". 
  2. If init is given, then [fill](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-fill) [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this) with init. 


The `append(name, value)` method steps are to [append](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-append) (name, value) to [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this). 
The `delete(name)` method steps are: 
  1. If [validating](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers-validate) (name, ``) for [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this) returns false, then return. 
Passing a dummy [header value](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#header-value) ought not to have any negative repercussions. 
  2. If [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-guard) is "`request-no-cors`", name is not a [no-CORS-safelisted request-header name](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#no-cors-safelisted-request-header-name), and name is not a [privileged no-CORS request-header name](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#privileged-no-cors-request-header-name), then return. 
  3. If [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-header-list) [does not contain](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#header-list-contains) name, then return. 
  4. [Delete](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-delete) name from [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-header-list). 
  5. If [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-guard) is "`request-no-cors`", then [remove privileged no-CORS request-headers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-remove-privileged-no-cors-request-headers) from [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this). 


The `get(name)` method steps are: 
  1. If name is not a [header name](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#header-name), then [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
  2. Return the result of [getting](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-get) name from [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-header-list). 


The `getSetCookie()` method steps are: 
  1. If [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-header-list) [does not contain](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#header-list-contains) ``Set-Cookie``, then return « ». 
  2. Return the [values](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-value) of all [headers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header) in [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-header-list) whose [name](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-name) is a [byte-case-insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-case-insensitive) match for ``Set-Cookie``, in order. 


The `has(name)` method steps are: 
  1. If name is not a [header name](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#header-name), then [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
  2. Return true if [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-header-list) [contains](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#header-list-contains) name; otherwise false. 


The `set(name, value)` method steps are: 
  1. [Normalize](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-value-normalize) value. 
  2. If [validating](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers-validate) (name, value) for [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this) returns false, then return. 
  3. If [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-guard) is "`request-no-cors`" and (name, value) is not a [no-CORS-safelisted request-header](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#no-cors-safelisted-request-header), then return. 
  4. [Set](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-set) (name, value) in [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-header-list). 
  5. If [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-guard) is "`request-no-cors`", then [remove privileged no-CORS request-headers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-remove-privileged-no-cors-request-headers) from [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this). 


The [value pairs to iterate over](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-value-pairs-to-iterate-over) are the return value of running [sort and combine](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-sort-and-combine) with [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-header-list). 
###  5.2. BodyInit unions

```
typedef ([Blob](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#dfn-Blob) or [BufferSource](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#BufferSource) or [FormData](https://xhr.spec.whatwg.org/#formdata) or [URLSearchParams](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#urlsearchparams) or [USVString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-USVString)) XMLHttpRequestBodyInit;

typedef ([ReadableStream](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream) or [XMLHttpRequestBodyInit](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#typedefdef-xmlhttprequestbodyinit)) BodyInit;
```

To safely extract a [body with type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#body-with-type) from a [byte sequence](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-sequence) or `BodyInit[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#bodyinit)` object object, run these steps: 
  1. If object is a `ReadableStream[](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream)` object, then: 
    1. [Assert](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert): object is neither [disturbed](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#is-readable-stream-disturbed) nor [locked](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-locked). 
  2. Return the result of [extracting](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-bodyinit-extract) object. 


The [safely extract](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#bodyinit-safely-extract) operation is a subset of the [extract](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-bodyinit-extract) operation that is guaranteed to not throw an exception. 
To extract a [body with type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#body-with-type) from a [byte sequence](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-sequence) or `BodyInit[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#bodyinit)` object object, with an optional boolean keepalive (default false), run these steps: 
  1. Let stream be null. 
  2. If object is a `ReadableStream[](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream)` object, then set stream to object. 
  3. Otherwise, if object is a `Blob[](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#dfn-Blob)` object, set stream to the result of running object’s [get stream](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#blob-get-stream). 
  4. Otherwise, set stream to a [new](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#new) `ReadableStream[](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream)` object, and [set up](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-set-up-with-byte-reading-support) stream with byte reading support. 
  5. [Assert](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert): stream is a `ReadableStream[](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream)` object. 
  6. Let action be null. 
  7. Let source be null. 
  8. Let length be null. 
  9. Let type be null. 
  10. Switch on object:  

`Blob[](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#dfn-Blob)` 
    
Set source to object. 
Set length to object’s `size[](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#dfn-size)`. 
If object’s `type[](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#dfn-type)` attribute is not the empty byte sequence, set type to its value.  

[byte sequence](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-sequence) 
    
Set source to object.  

`BufferSource[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#BufferSource)` 
    
Set source to a [copy of the bytes](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-get-buffer-source-copy) held by object.  

`FormData[](https://xhr.spec.whatwg.org/#formdata)` 
    
Set action to this step: run the [`multipart/form-data` encoding algorithm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/form-control-infrastructure.html#multipart%2Fform-data-encoding-algorithm), with object’s [entry list](https://xhr.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-formdata-entry-list) and [UTF-8](https://encoding.spec.whatwg.org/#utf-8). 
Set source to object. 
Set length to unclear, see [html/6424](https://github.com/whatwg/html/issues/6424) for improving this. 
Set type to ``multipart/form-data; boundary=``, followed by the [`multipart/form-data` boundary string](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/form-control-infrastructure.html#multipart%2Fform-data-boundary-string) generated by the [`multipart/form-data` encoding algorithm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/form-control-infrastructure.html#multipart%2Fform-data-encoding-algorithm).  

`URLSearchParams[](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#urlsearchparams)` 
    
Set source to the result of running the [`application/x-www-form-urlencoded` serializer](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-urlencoded-serializer) with object’s [list](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-urlsearchparams-list). 
Set type to ``application/x-www-form-urlencoded;charset=UTF-8``.  

[scalar value string](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#scalar-value-string) 
    
Set source to the [UTF-8 encoding](https://encoding.spec.whatwg.org/#utf-8-encode) of object. 
Set type to ``text/plain;charset=UTF-8``.  

`ReadableStream[](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream)` 
    
If keepalive is true, then [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
If object is [disturbed](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#is-readable-stream-disturbed) or [locked](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-locked), then [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
  11. If source is a [byte sequence](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-sequence), then set action to a step that returns source and length to source’s [length](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-sequence-length). 
  12. If action is non-null, then run these steps [in parallel](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#in-parallel): 
    1. Run action. 
Whenever one or more bytes are available and stream is not [errored](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-errored), [enqueue](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-enqueue) the result of [creating](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#arraybufferview-create) a `Uint8Array[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-Uint8Array)` from the available bytes into stream. 
When running action is done, [close](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-close) stream. 
  13. Let body be a [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body) whose [stream](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-stream) is stream, [source](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-source) is source, and [length](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-total-bytes) is length. 
  14. Return (body, type). 


###  5.3. Body mixin

```
interface mixin Body {
  readonly attribute [ReadableStream](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream)? [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-body-body);
  readonly attribute [boolean](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-boolean) [bodyUsed](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-body-bodyused);
  [[NewObject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#NewObject)] [Promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-promise)<[ArrayBuffer](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-ArrayBuffer)> [arrayBuffer](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-body-arraybuffer)();
  [[NewObject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#NewObject)] [Promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-promise)<[Blob](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#dfn-Blob)> [blob](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-body-blob)();
  [[NewObject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#NewObject)] [Promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-promise)<[Uint8Array](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-Uint8Array)> [bytes](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-body-bytes)();
  [[NewObject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#NewObject)] [Promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-promise)<[FormData](https://xhr.spec.whatwg.org/#formdata)> [formData](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-body-formdata)();
  [[NewObject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#NewObject)] [Promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-promise)<[any](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-any)> [json](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-body-json)();
  [[NewObject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#NewObject)] [Promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-promise)<[USVString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-USVString)> [text](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-body-text)();
```
