```
  [[NewObject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#NewObject)] [ReadableStream](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream) [textStream](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-body-textstream)();
};
```

Formats you would not want a network layer to be dependent upon, such as HTML, will likely not be exposed here. Rather, an HTML parser API might accept a stream in due course. 
Objects including the `Body[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#body)` interface mixin have an associated body (null or a [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body)). 
An object including the `Body[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#body)` interface mixin is said to be unusable if its [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-body) is non-null and its [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-body)’s [stream](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-stream) is [disturbed](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#is-readable-stream-disturbed) or [locked](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-locked). 
* * * 

`requestOrResponse . body[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-body-body)` 
    
Returns requestOrResponse’s body as `ReadableStream[](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream)`.  

`requestOrResponse . bodyUsed[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-body-bodyused)` 
    
Returns whether requestOrResponse’s body has been read from.  

`requestOrResponse . arrayBuffer[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-body-arraybuffer)()` 
    
Returns a promise fulfilled with requestOrResponse’s body as `ArrayBuffer[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-ArrayBuffer)`.  

`requestOrResponse . blob[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-body-blob)()` 
    
Returns a promise fulfilled with requestOrResponse’s body as `Blob[](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#dfn-Blob)`.  

`requestOrResponse . bytes[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-body-bytes)()` 
    
Returns a promise fulfilled with requestOrResponse’s body as `Uint8Array[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-Uint8Array)`.  

`requestOrResponse . formData[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-body-formdata)()` 
    
Returns a promise fulfilled with requestOrResponse’s body as `FormData[](https://xhr.spec.whatwg.org/#formdata)`.  

`requestOrResponse . json[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-body-json)()` 
    
Returns a promise fulfilled with requestOrResponse’s body parsed as JSON.  

`requestOrResponse . text[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-body-text)()` 
    
Returns a promise fulfilled with requestOrResponse’s body as string.  

`requestOrResponse . textStream[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-body-textstream)()` 
    
Returns a `ReadableStream[](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream)` object with requestOrResponse’s body as string chunks. 
* * *
To get the MIME type, given a `Request[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request)` or `Response[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response)` object requestOrResponse: 
  1. Let headers be null. 
  2. If requestOrResponse is a `Request[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request)` object, then set headers to requestOrResponse’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request)’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list). 
  3. Otherwise, set headers to requestOrResponse’s [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-response)’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list). 
  4. Let mimeType be the result of [extracting a MIME type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-extract-mime-type) from headers. 
  5. If mimeType is failure, then return null. 
  6. Return mimeType. 


The `body` getter steps are to return null if [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-body) is null; otherwise [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-body)’s [stream](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-stream). 
The `bodyUsed` getter steps are to return true if [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-body) is non-null and [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-body)’s [stream](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-stream) is [disturbed](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#is-readable-stream-disturbed); otherwise false. 
The consume body algorithm, given an object that includes `Body[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#body)` object and an algorithm that takes a [byte sequence](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-sequence) and returns a JavaScript value or throws an exception convertBytesToJSValue, runs these steps: 
  1. If object is [unusable](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#body-unusable), then return [a promise rejected with](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-promise-rejected-with) a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
  2. Let promise be [a new promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise). 
  3. Let errorSteps given error be to [reject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#reject) promise with error. 
  4. Let successSteps given a [byte sequence](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-sequence) data be to [resolve](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#resolve) promise with the result of running convertBytesToJSValue with data. If that threw an exception, then run errorSteps with that exception. 
  5. If object’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-body) is null, then run successSteps with an empty [byte sequence](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-sequence). 
  6. Otherwise, [fully read](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#body-fully-read) object’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-body) given successSteps, errorSteps, and object’s [relevant global object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-global). 
  7. Return promise. 


The `arrayBuffer()` method steps are to return the result of running [consume body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-consume-body) with [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this) and the following step given a [byte sequence](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-sequence) bytes: return the result of [creating](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#arraybuffer-create) an `ArrayBuffer[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-ArrayBuffer)` from bytes in [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [relevant realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm). 
The above method can reject with a `RangeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-rangeerror)`. 
The `blob()` method steps are to return the result of running [consume body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-consume-body) with [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this) and the following step given a [byte sequence](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-sequence) bytes: return a `Blob[](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#dfn-Blob)` whose contents are bytes and whose `type[](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#dfn-type)` attribute is the result of [get the MIME type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-mime-type) with [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this). 
The `bytes()` method steps are to return the result of running [consume body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-consume-body) with [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this) and the following step given a [byte sequence](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-sequence) bytes: return the result of [creating](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#arraybufferview-create) a `Uint8Array[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-Uint8Array)` from bytes in [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [relevant realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm). 
The above method can reject with a `RangeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-rangeerror)`. 
The `formData()` method steps are to return the result of running [consume body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-consume-body) with [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this) and the following steps given a [byte sequence](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-sequence) bytes: 
  1. Let mimeType be the result of [get the MIME type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-mime-type) with [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this). 
  2. If mimeType is non-null, then switch on mimeType’s [essence](https://mimesniff.spec.whatwg.org/#mime-type-essence) and run the corresponding steps:  

"`multipart/form-data`" 
    
    1. Parse bytes, using the value of the ``boundary`` parameter from mimeType, per the rules set forth in Returning Values from Forms: multipart/form-data. [[RFC7578]](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#biblio-rfc7578 "Returning Values from Forms: multipart/form-data")
Each part whose ``Content-Disposition`` header contains a ``filename`` parameter must be parsed into an [entry](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/form-control-infrastructure.html#form-entry) whose value is a `File[](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#dfn-file)` object whose contents are the contents of the part. The `name[](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#dfn-name)` attribute of the `File[](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#dfn-file)` object must have the value of the ``filename`` parameter of the part. The `type[](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#dfn-type)` attribute of the `File[](https://w3c.github.io/FileAPI/#dfn-file)` object must have the value of the ``Content-Type`` header of the part if the part has such header, and ``text/plain`` (the default defined by [[RFC7578]](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#biblio-rfc7578 "Returning Values from Forms: multipart/form-data") section 4.4) otherwise. 
Each part whose ``Content-Disposition`` header does not contain a ``filename`` parameter must be parsed into an [entry](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/form-control-infrastructure.html#form-entry) whose value is the [UTF-8 decoded without BOM](https://encoding.spec.whatwg.org/#utf-8-decode-without-bom) content of the part. This is done regardless of the presence or the value of a ``Content-Type`` header and regardless of the presence or the value of a ``charset`` parameter.
A part whose ``Content-Disposition`` header contains a ``name`` parameter whose value is ``_charset_`` is parsed like any other part. It does not change the encoding. 
    2. If that fails for some reason, then [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
    3. Return a new `FormData[](https://xhr.spec.whatwg.org/#formdata)` object, appending each [entry](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/form-control-infrastructure.html#form-entry), resulting from the parsing operation, to its [entry list](https://xhr.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-formdata-entry-list). 
The above is a rough approximation of what is needed for ``multipart/form-data``, a more detailed parsing specification is to be written. Volunteers welcome.  

"`application/x-www-form-urlencoded`" 
    
    1. Let entries be the result of [parsing](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-urlencoded-parser) bytes. 
    2. Return a new `FormData[](https://xhr.spec.whatwg.org/#formdata)` object whose [entry list](https://xhr.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-formdata-entry-list) is entries. 
  3. [Throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 


The `json()` method steps are to return the result of running [consume body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-consume-body) with [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this) and [parse JSON from bytes](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#parse-json-bytes-to-a-javascript-value). 
The above method can reject with a `SyntaxError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#syntaxerror)`. 
The `text()` method steps are to return the result of running [consume body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-consume-body) with [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this) and [UTF-8 decode](https://encoding.spec.whatwg.org/#utf-8-decode). 
The `textStream()` method steps are:
  1. If this is [unusable](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#body-unusable), then throw a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
  2. If this’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-body) is null: 
    1. Let emptyStream be a new `ReadableStream[](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream)` in [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [relevant realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm). 
    2. [Set up](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-set-up) emptyStream. 
    3. [Close](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-close) emptyStream. 
    4. Return emptyStream. 
  3. Let stream be [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-body)’s [stream](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-stream). 
  4. Let decoder be a new `TextDecoderStream[](https://encoding.spec.whatwg.org/#textdecoderstream)` object in [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [relevant realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm). 
  5. [Set up](https://encoding.spec.whatwg.org/#set-up-a-text-decoder-stream) decoder with [UTF-8](https://encoding.spec.whatwg.org/#utf-8). 
This is done regardless of the presence or the value of a ``Content-Type`` header and regardless of the presence or the value of a ``charset`` parameter.
  6. Return the result of stream, [piped through](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-pipe-through) decoder. 


###  5.4. Request class

```
typedef ([Request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request) or [USVString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-USVString)) RequestInfo;

[[Exposed](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#Exposed)=(Window,Worker)]
interface Request {
  [constructor](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request)([RequestInfo](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#requestinfo) input, optional [RequestInit](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#requestinit) init = {});

  readonly attribute [ByteString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-ByteString) [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-method);
  readonly attribute [USVString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-USVString) [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-url);
  [[SameObject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#SameObject)] readonly attribute [Headers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers) [headers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-headers);

  readonly attribute [RequestDestination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#requestdestination) [destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-destination);
  readonly attribute [USVString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-USVString) [referrer](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-referrer);
  readonly attribute [ReferrerPolicy](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-referrer-policy/#enumdef-referrerpolicy) [referrerPolicy](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-referrerpolicy);
  readonly attribute [RequestMode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#requestmode) [mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-mode);
  readonly attribute [RequestCredentials](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#requestcredentials) [credentials](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-credentials);
  readonly attribute [RequestCache](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#requestcache) [cache](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-cache);
  readonly attribute [RequestRedirect](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#requestredirect) [redirect](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-redirect);
  readonly attribute [DOMString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMString) [integrity](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-integrity);
  readonly attribute [boolean](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-boolean) [keepalive](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-keepalive);
  readonly attribute [boolean](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-boolean) [isReloadNavigation](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-isreloadnavigation);
  readonly attribute [boolean](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-boolean) [isHistoryNavigation](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-ishistorynavigation);
  readonly attribute [AbortSignal](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#abortsignal) [signal](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-signal);
  readonly attribute [RequestDuplex](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#enumdef-requestduplex) [duplex](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-duplex);

  [[NewObject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#NewObject)] [Request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request) [clone](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-clone)();
};
[Request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request) includes [Body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#body);

dictionary RequestInit {
  [ByteString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-ByteString) method;
  [HeadersInit](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#typedefdef-headersinit) headers;
  [BodyInit](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#bodyinit)? body;
  [USVString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-USVString) referrer;
  [ReferrerPolicy](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-referrer-policy/#enumdef-referrerpolicy) referrerPolicy;
  [RequestMode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#requestmode) mode;
  [RequestCredentials](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#requestcredentials) credentials;
  [RequestCache](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#requestcache) cache;
  [RequestRedirect](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#requestredirect) redirect;
  [DOMString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMString) integrity;
  [boolean](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-boolean) keepalive;
  [AbortSignal](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#abortsignal)? signal;
  [RequestDuplex](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#enumdef-requestduplex) duplex;
  [RequestPriority](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#enumdef-requestpriority) priority;
  [any](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-any) window; // can only be set to null
};

enum RequestDestination { "", "audio", "audioworklet", "document", "embed", "font", "frame", "iframe", "image", "json", "manifest", "object", "paintworklet", "report", "script", "sharedworker", "style", "text", "track", "video", "worker", "xslt" };
enum RequestMode { "navigate", "same-origin", "no-cors", "cors" };
enum RequestCredentials { "omit", "same-origin", "include" };
enum RequestCache { "default", "no-store", "reload", "no-cache", "force-cache", "only-if-cached" };
enum RequestRedirect { "follow", "error", "manual" };
enum RequestDuplex { "half" };
enum RequestPriority { "high", "low", "auto" };

```

"`serviceworker`" is omitted from [`RequestDestination`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#requestdestination) as it cannot be observed from JavaScript. Implementations will still need to support it as a [destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-destination). "`websocket`" and "`webtransport`" are omitted from [`RequestMode`](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#requestmode) as they cannot be used or observed from JavaScript. 
A `Request[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request)` object has an associated request (a [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request)). 
A `Request[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request)` object also has an associated headers (null or a `Headers[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers)` object), initially null. 
A `Request[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request)` object has an associated signal (null or an `AbortSignal[](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#abortsignal)` object), initially null. 
A `Request[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request)` object’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-body) is its [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request)’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body). 
* * * 

`request = new Request[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request)(input [,  init])` 
    
Returns a new request whose `url[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-url)` property is input if input is a string, and input’s `url[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-url)` if input is a `Request[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request)` object. 
The init argument is an object whose properties can be set as follows: 

`method[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-method)` 
    A string to set request’s `method[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-method)`.  

`headers[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-headers)` 
    A `Headers[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers)` object, an object literal, or an array of two-item arrays to set request’s `headers[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-headers)`.  

`body[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-body)` 
    A `BodyInit[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#bodyinit)` object or null to set request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body).  

`referrer[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-referrer)` 
    A string whose value is a same-origin URL, "`about:client`", or the empty string, to set request’s [referrer](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-referrer).  

`referrerPolicy[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-referrerpolicy)` 
    A [referrer policy](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-referrer-policy/#referrer-policy) to set request’s `referrerPolicy[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-referrerpolicy)`.  

`mode[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-mode)` 
    A string to indicate whether the request will use CORS, or will be restricted to same-origin URLs. Sets request’s `mode[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-mode)`. If input is a string, it defaults to "`cors`".  

`credentials[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-credentials)` 
    A string indicating whether credentials will be sent with the request always, never, or only when sent to a same-origin URL — as well as whether any credentials sent back in the response will be used always, never, or only when received from a same-origin URL. Sets request’s `credentials[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-credentials)`. If input is a string, it defaults to "`same-origin`".  

`cache[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-cache)` 
    A string indicating how the request will interact with the browser’s cache to set request’s `cache[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-cache)`.  

`redirect[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-redirect)` 
    A string indicating whether request follows redirects, results in an error upon encountering a redirect, or returns the redirect (in an opaque fashion). Sets request’s `redirect[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-redirect)`.  

`integrity[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-integrity)` 
    A cryptographic hash of the resource to be fetched by request. Sets request’s `integrity[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-integrity)`.  

`keepalive[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-keepalive)` 
    A boolean to set request’s `keepalive[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-keepalive)`.  

`signal[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-signal)` 
    An `AbortSignal[](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#abortsignal)` to set request’s `signal[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-signal)`.  

`window[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-window)` 
    Can only be null. Used to disassociate request from any `Window[](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window)`.  

`duplex[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-duplex)` 
    "`half`" is the only valid value and it is for initiating a half-duplex fetch (i.e., the user agent sends the entire request before processing the response). "`full`" is reserved for future use, for initiating a full-duplex fetch (i.e., the user agent can process the response before sending the entire request). This member needs to be set when `body[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-body)` is a `ReadableStream[](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream)` object. See [issue #1254](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch/issues/1254) for defining "`full`". 

`priority[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-priority)` 
    A string to set request’s [priority](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-priority).  

`request . method[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-method)` 
    Returns request’s HTTP method, which is "`GET`" by default.  

`request . url[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-url)` 
    Returns the URL of request as a string.  

`request . headers[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-headers)` 
    Returns a `Headers[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers)` object consisting of the headers associated with request. Note that headers added in the network layer by the user agent will not be accounted for in this object, e.g., the "`Host`" header.  

`request . destination[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-destination)` 
    Returns the kind of resource requested by request, e.g., "`document`" or "`script`".  

`request . referrer[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-referrer)` 
    Returns the referrer of request. Its value can be a same-origin URL if explicitly set in init, the empty string to indicate no referrer, and "`about:client`" when defaulting to the global’s default. This is used during fetching to determine the value of the ``Referer`` header of the request being made.  

`request . referrerPolicy[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-referrerpolicy)` 
    Returns the referrer policy associated with request. This is used during fetching to compute the value of the request’s referrer.  

`request . mode[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-mode)` 
    Returns the [mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-mode) associated with request, which is a string indicating whether the request will use CORS, or will be restricted to same-origin URLs.  

`request . credentials[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-credentials)` 
    Returns the [credentials mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-credentials-mode) associated with request, which is a string indicating whether credentials will be sent with the request always, never, or only when sent to a same-origin URL.  

`request . cache[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-cache)` 
    Returns the [cache mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-cache-mode) associated with request, which is a string indicating how the request will interact with the browser’s cache when fetching.  

`request . redirect[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-redirect)` 
    Returns the [redirect mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-redirect-mode) associated with request, which is a string indicating how redirects for the request will be handled during fetching. A [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request) will follow redirects by default.  

`request . integrity[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-integrity)` 
    Returns request’s subresource integrity metadata, which is a cryptographic hash of the resource being fetched. Its value consists of multiple hashes separated by whitespace. [[SRI]](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#biblio-sri "Subresource Integrity") 

`request . keepalive[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-keepalive)` 
    Returns a boolean indicating whether or not request can outlive the global in which it was created.  

`request . isReloadNavigation[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-isreloadnavigation)` 
    Returns a boolean indicating whether or not request is for a reload navigation.  

`request . isHistoryNavigation[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-ishistorynavigation)` 
    Returns a boolean indicating whether or not request is for a history navigation (a.k.a. back-foward navigation).  

`request . signal[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-signal)` 
    Returns the signal associated with request, which is an `AbortSignal[](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#abortsignal)` object indicating whether or not request has been aborted, and its abort event handler.  

`request . duplex[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-duplex)` 
    Returns "`half`", meaning the fetch will be half-duplex (i.e., the user agent sends the entire request before processing the response). In future, it could also return "`full`", meaning the fetch will be full-duplex (i.e., the user agent can process the response before sending the entire request) to indicate that the fetch will be full-duplex. See [issue #1254](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch/issues/1254) for defining "`full`". 

`request . clone[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-request-clone)()` 
    
Returns a clone of request. 
* * *
To create a `Request[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request)` object, given a [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request) request, [headers guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers-guard) guard, `AbortSignal[](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#abortsignal)` object signal, and [realm](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#realm) realm: 
  1. Let requestObject be a [new](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#new) `Request[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request)` object with realm. 
  2. Set requestObject’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request) to request. 
  3. Set requestObject’s [headers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-headers) to a [new](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#new) `Headers[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers)` object with realm, whose [headers list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-header-list) is request’s [headers list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list) and [guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-guard) is guard. 
  4. Set requestObject’s [signal](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-signal) to signal. 
  5. Return requestObject. 


* * *
The `new Request(input, init)` constructor steps are: 
  1. Let request be null. 
  2. Let fallbackMode be null. 
  3. Let baseURL be [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [relevant settings object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object)’s [API base URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#api-base-url). 
  4. Let signal be null. 
  5. If input is a string, then: 
    1. Let parsedURL be the result of [parsing](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-parser) input with baseURL. 
    2. If parsedURL is failure, then [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
    3. If parsedURL [includes credentials](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#include-credentials), then [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
    4. Set request to a new [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request) whose [URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url) is parsedURL. 
    5. Set fallbackMode to "`cors`". 
  6. Otherwise: 
    1. [Assert](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert): input is a `Request[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request)` object. 
    2. Set request to input’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request). 
    3. Set signal to input’s [signal](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-signal). 
  7. Let origin be [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [relevant settings object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object)’s [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin). 
  8. Let traversableForUserPrompts be "`client`". 
  9. If request’s [traversable for user prompts](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-window) is an [environment settings object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object) and its [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-origin) is [same origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#same-origin) with origin, then set traversableForUserPrompts to request’s [traversable for user prompts](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-window). 
  10. If init["`window[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-window)`"] [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists) and is non-null, then [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
  11. If init["`window[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-window)`"] [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then set traversableForUserPrompts to "`no-traversable`". 
  12. Set request to a new [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request) with the following properties:  

[URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url) 
     request’s [URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url).  

[method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method) 
     request’s [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method).  

[header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list) 
    A copy of request’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list).  

[unsafe-request flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#unsafe-request-flag) 
    Set.  

[client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client) 
     [This](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [relevant settings object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#relevant-settings-object).  

[traversable for user prompts](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-window) 
     traversableForUserPrompts.  

[internal priority](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-internal-priority) 
     request’s [internal priority](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-internal-priority).  

[origin](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-origin) 
     request’s [origin](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-origin). The propagation of the [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#concept-origin) is only significant for navigation requests being handled by a service worker. In this scenario a request can have an origin that is different from the current client. 

[referrer](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-referrer) 
     request’s [referrer](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-referrer).  

[referrer policy](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-referrer-policy) 
     request’s [referrer policy](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-referrer-policy).  

[mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-mode) 
     request’s [mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-mode).  

[credentials mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-credentials-mode) 
     request’s [credentials mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-credentials-mode).  

[cache mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-cache-mode) 
     request’s [cache mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-cache-mode).  

[redirect mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-redirect-mode) 
     request’s [redirect mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-redirect-mode).  

[integrity metadata](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-integrity-metadata) 
     request’s [integrity metadata](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-integrity-metadata).  

[keepalive](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-keepalive-flag) 
     request’s [keepalive](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-keepalive-flag).  

[reload-navigation flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-reload-navigation-flag) 
     request’s [reload-navigation flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-reload-navigation-flag).  

[history-navigation flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-history-navigation-flag) 
     request’s [history-navigation flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-history-navigation-flag).  

[URL list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url-list) 
    A [clone](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-clone) of request’s [URL list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url-list).  

[initiator type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-initiator-type) 
    "`fetch`". 
  13. If init [is not empty](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-is-empty), then: 
    1. If request’s [mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-mode) is "`navigate`", then set it to "`same-origin`". 
    2. Unset request’s [reload-navigation flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-reload-navigation-flag). 
    3. Unset request’s [history-navigation flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-history-navigation-flag). 
    4. Set request’s [origin](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-origin) to "`client`". 
    5. Set request’s [referrer](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-referrer) to "`client`". 
    6. Set request’s [referrer policy](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-referrer-policy) to the empty string. 
    7. Set request’s [URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url) to request’s [current URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-current-url). 
    8. Set request’s [URL list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url-list) to « request’s [URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url) ». 
This is done to ensure that when a service worker "redirects" a request, e.g., from an image in a cross-origin style sheet, and makes modifications, it no longer appears to come from the original source (i.e., the cross-origin style sheet), but instead from the service worker that "redirected" the request. This is important as the original source might not even be able to generate the same kind of requests as the service worker. Services that trust the original source could therefore be exploited were this not done, although that is somewhat farfetched. 
  14. If init["`referrer[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-referrer)`"] [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then: 
    1. Let referrer be init["`referrer[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-referrer)`"]. 
    2. If referrer is the empty string, then set request’s [referrer](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-referrer) to "`no-referrer`". 
    3. Otherwise: 
      1. Let parsedReferrer be the result of [parsing](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-parser) referrer with baseURL. 
      2. If parsedReferrer is failure, then [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
      3. If one of the following is true 
         * parsedReferrer’s [scheme](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-scheme) is "`about`" and [path](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-path) is the string "`client`" 
         * parsedReferrer’s [origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin) is not [same origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/browsers.html#same-origin) with origin
then set request’s [referrer](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-referrer) to "`client`". 
      4. Otherwise, set request’s [referrer](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-referrer) to parsedReferrer. 
  15. If init["`referrerPolicy[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-referrerpolicy)`"] [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then set request’s [referrer policy](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-referrer-policy) to it. 
  16. Let mode be init["`mode[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-mode)`"] if it [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), and fallbackMode otherwise. 
  17. If mode is "`navigate`", then [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
  18. If mode is non-null, set request’s [mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-mode) to mode. 
  19. If init["`credentials[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-credentials)`"] [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then set request’s [credentials mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-credentials-mode) to it. 
  20. If init["`cache[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-cache)`"] [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then set request’s [cache mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-cache-mode) to it. 
  21. If request’s [cache mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-cache-mode) is "`only-if-cached`" and request’s [mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-mode) is _not_ "`same-origin`", then [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
  22. If init["`redirect[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-redirect)`"] [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then set request’s [redirect mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-redirect-mode) to it. 
  23. If init["`integrity[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-integrity)`"] [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then set request’s [integrity metadata](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-integrity-metadata) to it. 
  24. If init["`keepalive[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-keepalive)`"] [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then set request’s [keepalive](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-keepalive-flag) to it. 
  25. If init["`method[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-method)`"] [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then: 
    1. Let method be init["`method[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-method)`"]. 
    2. If method is not a [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-method) or method is a [forbidden method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#forbidden-method), then [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
    3. [Normalize](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-method-normalize) method. 
    4. Set request’s [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method) to method. 
  26. If init["`signal[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-signal)`"] [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then set signal to it. 
  27. If init["`priority[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-priority)`"] [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then: 
    1. If request’s [internal priority](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-internal-priority) is not null, then update request’s [internal priority](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-internal-priority) in an [implementation-defined](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#implementation-defined) manner. 
    2. Otherwise, set request’s [priority](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-priority) to init["`priority[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-priority)`"]. 
  28. Set [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request) to request. 
  29. Let signals be « signal » if signal is non-null; otherwise « ». 
  30. Set [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [signal](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-signal) to the result of [creating a dependent abort signal](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#create-a-dependent-abort-signal) from signals, using `AbortSignal[](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#abortsignal)` and [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [relevant realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm). 
  31. Set [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [headers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-headers) to a [new](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#new) `Headers[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers)` object with [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [relevant realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm), whose [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-header-list) is request’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-header-list) and [guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-guard) is "`request`". 
  32. If [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request)’s [mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-mode) is "`no-cors`", then: 
    1. If [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request)’s [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method) is not a [CORS-safelisted method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#cors-safelisted-method), then [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
    2. Set [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [headers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-headers)’s [guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-guard) to "`request-no-cors`". 
  33. If init [is not empty](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-is-empty), then: 
The headers are sanitized as they might contain headers that are not allowed by this mode. Otherwise, they were previously sanitized or are unmodified since they were set by a privileged API. 
    1. Let headers be a copy of [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [headers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-headers) and its associated [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-header-list). 
    2. If init["`headers[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-headers)`"] [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then set headers to init["`headers[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-headers)`"]. 
    3. Empty [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [headers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-headers)’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-header-list). 
    4. If headers is a `Headers[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers)` object, then [for each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) header of its [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-header-list), [append](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-append) header to [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [headers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-headers). 
    5. Otherwise, [fill](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-fill) [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [headers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-headers) with headers. 
  34. Let inputBody be input’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request)’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) if input is a `Request[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request)` object; otherwise null. 
  35. If either init["`body[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-body)`"] [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists) and is non-null or inputBody is non-null, and request’s [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method) is ``GET`` or ``HEAD``, then [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
  36. Let initBody be null. 
  37. If init["`body[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-body)`"] [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists) and is non-null, then: 
    1. Let bodyWithType be the result of [extracting](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-bodyinit-extract) init["`body[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-body)`"], with [_keepalive_](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#keepalive) set to request’s [keepalive](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-keepalive-flag). 
    2. Set initBody to bodyWithType’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#body-with-type-body). 
    3. Let type be bodyWithType’s [type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#body-with-type-type). 
    4. If type is non-null and [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [headers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-headers)’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-header-list) [does not contain](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#header-list-contains) ``Content-Type``, then [append](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-append) (``Content-Type``, type) to [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [headers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-headers). 
  38. Let inputOrInitBody be initBody if it is non-null; otherwise inputBody. 
  39. If inputOrInitBody is non-null and inputOrInitBody’s [source](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-source) is null, then: 
    1. If initBody is non-null and init["`duplex[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-requestinit-duplex)`"] does not [exist](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then throw a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
    2. If [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request)’s [mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-mode) is neither "`same-origin`" nor "`cors`", then throw a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
    3. Set [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request)’s [use-CORS-preflight flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#use-cors-preflight-flag). 
  40. Let finalBody be inputOrInitBody. 
  41. If initBody is null and inputBody is non-null, then: 
    1. If inputBody is [unusable](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#body-unusable), then [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
    2. Set finalBody to the result of [creating a proxy](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-create-a-proxy) for inputBody. 
  42. Set [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request)’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) to finalBody. 


The `method` getter steps are to return [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request)’s [method](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-method). 
The `url` getter steps are to return [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request)’s [URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-url), [serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer). 
The `headers` getter steps are to return [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [headers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-headers). 
The `destination` getter are to return [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request)’s [destination](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-destination). 
The `referrer` getter steps are: 
  1. If [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request)’s [referrer](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-referrer) is "`no-referrer`", then return the empty string. 
  2. If [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request)’s [referrer](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-referrer) is "`client`", then return "`about:client`". 
  3. Return [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request)’s [referrer](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-referrer), [serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer). 


The `referrerPolicy` getter steps are to return [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request)’s [referrer policy](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-referrer-policy). 
The `mode` getter steps are to return [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request)’s [mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-mode). 
The `credentials` getter steps are to return [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request)’s [credentials mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-credentials-mode). 
The `cache` getter steps are to return [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request)’s [cache mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-cache-mode). 
The `redirect` getter steps are to return [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request)’s [redirect mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-redirect-mode). 
The `integrity` getter steps are to return [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request)’s [integrity metadata](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-integrity-metadata). 
The `keepalive` getter steps are to return [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request)’s [keepalive](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-keepalive-flag). 
The `isReloadNavigation` getter steps are to return true if [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request)’s [reload-navigation flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-reload-navigation-flag) is set; otherwise false. 
The `isHistoryNavigation` getter steps are to return true if [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request)’s [history-navigation flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-history-navigation-flag) is set; otherwise false. 
The `signal` getter steps are to return [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [signal](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-signal). 
[This](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [signal](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-signal) is always initialized in the [constructor](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#signal-initialized-in-constructor) and when [cloning](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#signal-initialized-when-cloning). 
The `duplex` getter steps are to return "`half`". 
* * *
The `clone()` method steps are: 
  1. If [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this) is [unusable](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#body-unusable), then [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
  2. Let clonedRequest be the result of [cloning](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-clone) [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request). 
  3. [Assert](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert): [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [signal](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-signal) is non-null. 
  4. Let clonedSignal be the result of [creating a dependent abort signal](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#create-a-dependent-abort-signal) from « [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [signal](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-signal) », using `AbortSignal[](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#abortsignal)` and [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [relevant realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm). 
  5. Let clonedRequestObject be the result of [creating](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-create) a `Request[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request)` object, given clonedRequest, [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [headers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-headers)’s [guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-guard), clonedSignal and [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [relevant realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm). 
  6. Return clonedRequestObject. 


###  5.5. Response class

```
[[Exposed](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#Exposed)=(Window,Worker)]
interface Response {
  [constructor](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-response)(optional [BodyInit](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#bodyinit)? body = null, optional [ResponseInit](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#responseinit) init = {});

  [[NewObject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#NewObject)] static [Response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response) [error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-response-error)();
  [[NewObject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#NewObject)] static [Response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response) [redirect](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-response-redirect)([USVString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-USVString) url, optional [unsigned short](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-unsigned-short) status = 302);
  [[NewObject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#NewObject)] static [Response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response) [json](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-response-json)([any](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-any) data, optional [ResponseInit](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#responseinit) init = {});

  readonly attribute [ResponseType](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#responsetype) [type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-response-type);

  readonly attribute [USVString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-USVString) [url](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-response-url);
  readonly attribute [boolean](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-boolean) [redirected](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-response-redirected);
  readonly attribute [unsigned short](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-unsigned-short) [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-response-status);
  readonly attribute [boolean](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-boolean) [ok](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-response-ok);
  readonly attribute [ByteString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-ByteString) [statusText](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-response-statustext);
  [[SameObject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#SameObject)] readonly attribute [Headers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers) [headers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-response-headers);

  [[NewObject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#NewObject)] [Response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response) [clone](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-response-clone)();
};
[Response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response) includes [Body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#body);

dictionary ResponseInit {
  [unsigned short](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-unsigned-short) status = 200;
  [ByteString](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-ByteString) statusText = "";
  [HeadersInit](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#typedefdef-headersinit) headers;
};

enum ResponseType { "basic", "cors", "default", "error", "opaque", "opaqueredirect" };

```

A `Response[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response)` object has an associated response (a [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response)). 
A `Response[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response)` object also has an associated headers (null or a `Headers[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers)` object), initially null. 
A `Response[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response)` object’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-body-body) is its [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-response)’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body). 
* * * 

`response = new Response[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-response)(body = null [, init])` 
    
Creates a `Response[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response)` whose body is body, and status, status message, and headers are provided by init.  

`response = Response[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response) . error[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-response-error)()` 
    
Creates network error `Response[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response)`.  

`response = Response[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response) . redirect[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-response-redirect)(url, status = 302)` 
    
Creates a redirect `Response[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response)` that redirects to url with status status.  

`response = Response[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response) . json[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-response-json)(data [, init])` 
    
Creates a `Response[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response)` whose body is the JSON-encoded data, and status, status message, and headers are provided by init.  

`response . type[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-response-type)` 
    
Returns response’s type, e.g., "`cors`".  

`response . url[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-response-url)` 
    
Returns response’s URL, if it has one; otherwise the empty string.  

`response . redirected[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-response-redirected)` 
    
Returns whether response was obtained through a redirect.  

`response . status[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-response-status)` 
    
Returns response’s status.  

`response . ok[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-response-ok)` 
    
Returns whether response’s status is an [ok status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#ok-status).  

`response . statusText[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-response-statustext)` 
    
Returns response’s status message.  

`response . headers[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-response-headers)` 
    
Returns response’s headers as `Headers[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers)`.  

`response . clone[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-response-clone)()` 
    
Returns a clone of response. 
* * *
To create a `Response[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response)` object, given a [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response) response, [headers guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers-guard) guard, and [realm](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#realm) realm, run these steps: 
  1. Let responseObject be a [new](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#new) `Response[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response)` object with realm. 
  2. Set responseObject’s [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-response) to response. 
  3. Set responseObject’s [headers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response-headers) to a [new](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#new) `Headers[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers)` object with realm, whose [headers list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-header-list) is response’s [headers list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list) and [guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-guard) is guard. 
  4. Return responseObject. 


To initialize a response, given a `Response[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response)` object response, `ResponseInit[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#responseinit)` init, and null or a [body with type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#body-with-type) body: 
  1. If init["`status[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-responseinit-status)`"] is not in the range 200 to 599, inclusive, then [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `RangeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-rangeerror)`. 
  2. If init["`statusText[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-responseinit-statustext)`"] is not the empty string and does not match the [reason-phrase](https://httpwg.org/specs/rfc9112.html#status.line) token production, then [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
  3. Set response’s [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-response)’s [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status) to init["`status[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-responseinit-status)`"]. 
  4. Set response’s [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-response)’s [status message](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status-message) to init["`statusText[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-responseinit-statustext)`"]. 
  5. If init["`headers[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-responseinit-headers)`"] [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists), then [fill](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-fill) response’s [headers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response-headers) with init["`headers[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-responseinit-headers)`"]. 
  6. If body is non-null, then: 
    1. If response’s [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status) is a [null body status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#null-body-status), then [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
101 and 103 are included in [null body status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#null-body-status) due to their use elsewhere. They do not affect this step. 
    2. Set response’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-body) to body’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#body-with-type-body). 
    3. If body’s [type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#body-with-type-type) is non-null and response’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list) [does not contain](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#header-list-contains) ``Content-Type``, then [append](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-append) (``Content-Type``, body’s [type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#body-with-type-type)) to response’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list). 


* * *
The `new Response(body, init)` constructor steps are: 
  1. Set [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-response) to a new [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response). 
  2. Set [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [headers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response-headers) to a [new](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#new) `Headers[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#headers)` object with [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [relevant realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm), whose [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-header-list) is [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-response)’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list) and [guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-guard) is "`response`". 
  3. Let bodyWithType be null. 
  4. If body is non-null, then set bodyWithType to the result of [extracting](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-bodyinit-extract) body. 
  5. Perform [initialize a response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#initialize-a-response) given [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this), init, and bodyWithType. 


The static `error()` method steps are to return the result of [creating](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response-create) a `Response[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response)` object, given a new [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error), "`immutable`", and the [current realm](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#current-realm). 
The static `redirect(url, status)` method steps are: 
  1. Let parsedURL be the result of [parsing](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-parser) url with [current settings object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#current-settings-object)’s [API base URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#api-base-url). 
  2. If parsedURL is failure, then [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
  3. If status is not a [redirect status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#redirect-status), then [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `RangeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-rangeerror)`. 
  4. Let responseObject be the result of [creating](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response-create) a `Response[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response)` object, given a new [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response), "`immutable`", and the [current realm](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#current-realm). 
  5. Set responseObject’s [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-response)’s [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status) to status. 
  6. Let value be parsedURL, [serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer) and [isomorphic encoded](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#isomorphic-encode). 
  7. [Append](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-append) (``Location``, value) to responseObject’s [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-response)’s [header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list). 
  8. Return responseObject. 


The static `json(data, init)` method steps are: 
  1. Let bytes the result of running [serialize a JavaScript value to JSON bytes](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#serialize-a-javascript-value-to-json-bytes) on data. 
  2. Let body be the result of [extracting](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-bodyinit-extract) bytes. 
  3. Let responseObject be the result of [creating](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response-create) a `Response[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response)` object, given a new [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response), "`response`", and the [current realm](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#current-realm). 
  4. Perform [initialize a response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#initialize-a-response) given responseObject, init, and (body, "`application/json`"). 
  5. Return responseObject. 


The `type` getter steps are to return [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-response)’s [type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-type). 
The `url` getter steps are to return the empty string if [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-response)’s [URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-url) is null; otherwise [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-response)’s [URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-url), [serialized](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer) with [_exclude fragment_](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#url-serializer-exclude-fragment) set to true. 
The `redirected` getter steps are to return true if [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-response)’s [URL list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-url-list)’s [size](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-size) is greater than 1; otherwise false. 
To filter out [responses](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response) that are the result of a redirect, do this directly through the API, e.g., `fetch(url, { redirect:"error" })`. This way a potentially unsafe [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response) cannot accidentally leak. 
The `status` getter steps are to return [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-response)’s [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status). 
The `ok` getter steps are to return true if [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-response)’s [status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status) is an [ok status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#ok-status); otherwise false. 
The `statusText` getter steps are to return [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-response)’s [status message](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status-message). 
The `headers` getter steps are to return [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [headers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response-headers). 
* * *
The `clone()` method steps are: 
  1. If [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this) is [unusable](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#body-unusable), then [throw](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw) a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)`. 
  2. Let clonedResponse be the result of [cloning](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-clone) [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-response). 
  3. Return the result of [creating](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response-create) a `Response[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response)` object, given clonedResponse, [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [headers](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response-headers)’s [guard](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-headers-guard), and [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [relevant realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm). 


###  5.6. Fetch methods

```
partial interface mixin [WindowOrWorkerGlobalScope](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#windoworworkerglobalscope) {
  [[NewObject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#NewObject)] [Promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-promise)<[Response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response)> [fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-global-fetch)([RequestInfo](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#requestinfo) input, optional [RequestInit](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#requestinit) init = {});
};

dictionary DeferredRequestInit : [RequestInit](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#requestinit) {
  [DOMHighResTimeStamp](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp) activateAfter;
};

[[Exposed](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#Exposed)=Window]
interface FetchLaterResult {
  readonly attribute [boolean](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-boolean) [activated](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-fetchlaterresult-activated);
};

partial interface [Window](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#window) {
  [[NewObject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#NewObject), [SecureContext](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#SecureContext)] [FetchLaterResult](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetchlaterresult) [fetchLater](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-window-fetchlater)([RequestInfo](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#requestinfo) input, optional [DeferredRequestInit](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#dictdef-deferredrequestinit) init = {});
};

```

The `fetch(input, init)` method steps are: 
  1. Let p be [a new promise](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#a-new-promise). 
  2. Let requestObject be the result of invoking the initial value of `Request[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request)` as constructor with input and init as arguments. If this throws an exception, [reject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#reject) p with it and return p. 
  3. Let request be requestObject’s [request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-request). 
  4. If requestObject’s [signal](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-signal) is [aborted](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#abortsignal-aborted), then: 
    1. [Abort the `fetch()` call](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#abort-fetch) with p, request, null, and requestObject’s [signal](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-signal)’s [abort reason](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#abortsignal-abort-reason). 
    2. Return p. 
  5. Let globalObject be request’s [client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client)’s [global object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global). 
  6. If globalObject is a `ServiceWorkerGlobalScope[](https://w3c.github.io/ServiceWorker/#serviceworkerglobalscope)` object, then set request’s [service-workers mode](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-service-workers-mode) to "`none`". 
  7. Let responseObject be null. 
  8. Let relevantRealm be [this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)’s [relevant realm](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-realm). 
  9. Let locallyAborted be false. 
This lets us reject promises with predictable timing, when the request to abort comes from the same thread as the call to fetch. 
  10. Let controller be null. 
  11. [Add the following abort steps](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#abortsignal-add) to requestObject’s [signal](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-signal): 
    1. Set locallyAborted to true. 
    2. [Assert](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert): controller is non-null. 
    3. [Abort](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-abort) controller with requestObject’s [signal](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-signal)’s [abort reason](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#abortsignal-abort-reason). 
    4. [Abort the `fetch()` call](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#abort-fetch) with p, request, responseObject, and requestObject’s [signal](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-signal)’s [abort reason](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#abortsignal-abort-reason). 
  12. Set controller to the result of calling [fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch) given request and [_processResponse_](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#process-response) given response being these steps: 
    1. If locallyAborted is true, then abort these steps. 
    2. If response’s [aborted flag](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-aborted) is set, then: 
      1. Let deserializedError be the result of [deserialize a serialized abort reason](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#deserialize-a-serialized-abort-reason) given controller’s [serialized abort reason](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#fetch-controller-serialized-abort-reason) and relevantRealm. 
      2. [Abort the `fetch()` call](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#abort-fetch) with p, request, responseObject, and deserializedError. 
      3. Abort these steps. 
    3. If response is a [network error](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-network-error), then [reject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#reject) p with a `TypeError[](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror)` and abort these steps. 
    4. Set responseObject to the result of [creating](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response-create) a `Response[](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#response)` object, given response, "`immutable`", and relevantRealm. 
    5. [Resolve](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#resolve) p with responseObject. 
  13. Return p. 


To abort a `fetch()` call with a promise, request, responseObject, and an error: 
  1. [Reject](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#reject) promise with error. 
This is a no-op if promise has already fulfilled. 
  2. If request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) is non-null and is [readable](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-readable), then [cancel](https://streams.spec.whatwg.org/#readablestream-cancel) request’s [body](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-body) with error. 
  3. If responseObject is null, then return.
